   219	  test("Content-Type, Content-Length, and chunked transfer abuse give typed 4xx errors") {
   220	    withWorld() { w =>
   221	      val log = CaptureLog()
   222	      w.query(log.log) { c =>
   223	        val body = queryJson()
   224	        Vector(Seq(), Seq("Content-Type" -> "text/plain"), Seq("Content-Type" -> "application/xml"))
   225	          .foreach { ct =>
   226	            val r = c.send(
   227	              "POST",
   228	              "/v1/query",
   229	              Some(BodyPublishers.ofString(body)),
   230	              Seq("X-Api-Key" -> ApiKey) ++ ct
   231	            )
   232	            clientError(r, s"content type $ct", Set("INVALID_FIELD"))
   233	          }
   234	        // A chunked body (no Content-Length) larger than the cap: REQUEST_TOO_LARGE.
   235	        val big = queryJson("Who is " + "a" * (w.cfg.services.maxBodyBytes + 10))
   236	        val chunked =
   237	          c.send(
   238	            "POST",
   239	            "/v1/query",
   240	            Some(BodyPublishers.ofInputStream(() => ByteArrayInputStream(big.getBytes(UTF_8))))
   241	          )
   242	        clientError(chunked, "chunked oversize", Set("REQUEST_TOO_LARGE"))
   243	        // A Content-Length shorter than the body: the server reads only that much.
   244	        val short = s"POST /v1/query HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nX-Api-Key: $ApiKey\r\n" +
   245	          "Content-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: 5\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n" + body
   246	        val (s1, r1) = RawHttp.text(c.port, short)
   247	        assert(s1 == 400, r1)
   248	        assert(c.get("/healthz", Seq()).status == 200)
   249	      }
   250	    }
   251	  }
   252	
   253	  test(
   254	    "a declared body that never arrives does not hold the exchange open (refused, 401, or unread)"
   255	  ) {
   256	    withWorld() { w =>
   257	      val log = CaptureLog()
   258	      w.query(log.log) { c =>
   259	        // The client declares a body and sends little or none of it. The service must still
   260	        // complete its response and end the exchange; it must not hold an HTTP thread waiting for
   261	        // (or draining) a body it refused or never reads. The 5 s socket timeout is only a hang
   262	        // guard: a healthy service answers in milliseconds. Each case is (request, status).
   263	        def req(head: String, length: Long, sent: String) =
   264	          s"$head HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\n" +
   265	            s"Content-Length: $length\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n$sent"
   266	        val cases = Vector(
   267	          "over the cap, with the key" -> (
   268	            req(s"POST /v1/query", 999999999L, queryJson())
   269	              .replace("Host:", s"X-Api-Key: $ApiKey\r\nHost:"),
   270	            400
   271	          ),
   272	          "over the cap, no key" -> (req("POST /v1/query", 999999999L, queryJson()), 401),
   273	          "unread body on /healthz, no key" -> (req("GET /healthz", 100L, ""), 200)
   274	        )
   275	        val stuck = cases.flatMap { case (name, (request, expected)) =>
   276	          scala.util.Try(RawHttp.text(c.port, request, timeoutMs = 5000)) match
   277	            case scala.util.Success((s, _)) if s == expected => None
   278	            case scala.util.Success((s, raw)) => Some(s"$name: status $s ${raw.take(120)}")
   279	            case scala.util.Failure(e)        => Some(s"$name: ${e.getClass.getSimpleName}")
   280	        }
   281	        assert(stuck.isEmpty, s"exchanges not completed: ${stuck.mkString("; ")}")
   282	      }
   283	    }
   284	  }
   285	
   286	  /** Reads until the server closes the connection; None when the hang guard passes first. */
   287	  private def untilClosed(port: Int, request: String, guardMs: Int): Option[String] =
   288	    val s = java.net.Socket()
   289	    try
   290	      s.connect(java.net.InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", port), 10000)
   291	      s.getOutputStream.write(request.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1))
   292	      s.getOutputStream.flush()
   293	      s.setSoTimeout(guardMs)
   294	      Some(String(s.getInputStream.readAllBytes(), UTF_8))
   295	    catch
   296	      case _: java.net.SocketTimeoutException => None
   297	      case _: java.net.SocketException        => Some("")
   298	    finally s.close()
   299	
   300	  test(
   301	    "repair-01 D5 on graph-evidence-api: an unread body that never comes frees the only thread; Connection: close"
   302	  ) {
   303	    // Work order 4: D5 was found and fixed on query-api; graph-evidence-api shares HttpService. With
   304	    // one HTTP thread, an exchange still waiting for the body would hold every later request. The
   305	    // 10 s guard is a hang detector only (the drain bound is HttpService.UnreadBodyDrainMs).
   306	    withWorld("services.graph-evidence-api.threads = 1") { w =>
   307	      val log = CaptureLog()
   308	      w.graphEvidence(log.log) { c =>
   309	        val node = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(ada, UTF_8)
   310	        Vector(
   311	          s"GET /v1/graph/nodes/$node HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nX-Api-Key: $ApiKey\r\n" +
   312	            "Content-Length: 100\r\n\r\n",
   313	          s"GET /v1/evidence/ev-${"a" * 32} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nX-Api-Key: $ApiKey\r\n" +
   314	            "Content-Length: 100\r\n\r\n",
   315	          "GET /healthz HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Length: 100\r\n\r\n",
   316	          "GET /v1/graph/nodes/x HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Length: 100\r\n\r\n"
   317	        ).foreach { req =>
   318	          val raw = untilClosed(c.port, req, 10000)
   319	          val what = req.linesIterator.next()
   320	          assert(raw.isDefined, s"$what: the connection was held open")
   321	          val head = raw.get.linesIterator.takeWhile(_.nonEmpty).toVector
   322	          assert(head.headOption.exists(_.startsWith("HTTP/1.1 ")), s"$what: $head")
   323	          assert(head.exists(_.equalsIgnoreCase("Connection: close")), s"$what: $head")
   324	          // The single thread is free again: a normal request answers.
   325	          assert(c.get("/healthz", Seq()).status == 200, what)
   326	        }
   327	      }
   328	    }
   329	  }
   330	
   331	  test("repair-01 D5: starting any service sets the JDK request read timeout for this JVM") {
   332	    // HttpService.limitRequestReads runs before the first JDK server; the tests JVM sets no
   333	    // -Dsun.net.httpserver.maxReqTime, so the services' own value must be in force.
   334	    withWorld() { w =>
   335	      w.graphEvidence(CaptureLog().log)(c => assert(c.get("/healthz", Seq()).status == 200))
   336	      assert(
   337	        sys.props
   338	          .get("sun.net.httpserver.maxReqTime")
   339	          .contains(scholarchain.services.HttpService.RequestReadTimeoutSeconds.toString)
   340	      )
   341	      assert(scholarchain.services.HttpService.RequestReadTimeoutSeconds == 30)
   342	    }
   343	  }
   344	
   345	  test(
   346	    "Idempotency-Key and request-ID headers: bounded, visible ASCII, never echoed when invalid"
   347	  ) {
   348	    withWorld() { w =>
   349	      val log = CaptureLog()
   350	      w.query(log.log) { c =>
   351	        Vector("a" * 256, "has space", "two  spaces")
   352	          .foreach(k =>
   353	            clientError(
   354	              c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> k)),
   355	              k.take(20),
   356	              Set("INVALID_FIELD")
   357	            )
   358	          )
   359	        assert(
   360	          c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> ("k" * 255))).status == 200
   361	        )
   362	        // Invalid client IDs are replaced, so they never reach a header, a body, or a log line.
   363	        val marker = "SYNTHETIC-REQID-<script>alert(1)</script>"
   364	        val r = c.get("/healthz", Seq("X-Request-Id" -> marker, "X-Trace-Id" -> ("t" * 129)))
   365	        assert(r.status == 200)
   366	        assert(!r.headers.values.exists(v => v.contains("script") || v == "t" * 129))
   367	        assert(
   368	          r.headers("X-Request-Id").startsWith("req-") && r
   369	            .headers("X-Trace-Id")
   370	            .startsWith("trace-")
   371	        )
   372	      }
   373	      assert(!log.all.exists(l => l.contains("SYNTHETIC-REQID") || l.contains("t" * 129)))
   374	    }
   375	  }

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/adversarial/scholarchain/tests/adversarial/BlockingTeamAdversarialSpec.scala (171 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.adversarial
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.*
     5	import scholarchain.entityresolution.{BlockingKeys, OversizePolicy}
     6	import scholarchain.hadoop.{Blocking, BlockMember, Teams}
     7	import upickle.default.write
     8	
     9	/** Phase 11 WO3, spec categories "very large blocking groups" and "large-team pair expansion" (the
    10	  * J05 and J07+J08 pure logic, with no Hadoop runtime), plus the common-name split rule: an
    11	  * oversize block is split or reported, never merged, and no member disappears silently.
    12	  */
    13	class BlockingTeamAdversarialSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    14	  private val run = StableIds.runId("adv", "code", "config-v1", Seq("dblp=2026-10-03"), 1)
    15	  private val prov =
    16	    Provenance(SchemaVersions.Provenance, SourceType.DBLP, "k", "2026-10-03", run, None, None)
    17	
    18	  private def work(i: Int, names: Seq[String], year: Int = 2020): WorkRecord =
    19	    val id = s"dblp:conf/adv/W$i"
    20	    WorkRecord(
    21	      SchemaVersions.WorkRecord,
    22	      id,
    23	      SourceType.DBLP,
    24	      s"conf/adv/W$i",
    25	      Some(s"Paper $i"),
    26	      Some(s"paper $i"),
    27	      Some(year),
    28	      None,
    29	      Some("inproceedings"),
    30	      None,
    31	      None,
    32	      Map.empty,
    33	      names.zipWithIndex
    34	        .map((n, k) =>
    35	          AuthorMention(
    36	            SchemaVersions.AuthorMention,
    37	            s"$id#${k + 1}",
    38	            id,
    39	            k + 1,
    40	            n,
    41	            n.toLowerCase,
    42	            None,
    43	            None,
    44	            Vector.empty,
    45	            prov
    46	          )
    47	        )
    48	        .toVector,
    49	      None,
    50	      prov
    51	    )
    52	
    53	  private def member(w: WorkRecord, pos: Int): BlockMember =
    54	    Blocking.members(w).find(_.position.contains(pos)).get
    55	
    56	  test(
    57	    "a 5,000-member common-name group at the last level is reported oversize with no member held or merged"
    58	  ) {
    59	    val members = (1 to 5000).map(i => member(work(i, Seq("Wei Wang")), 1))
    60	    val rekeyed = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
    61	    val (g, size, dupes) =
    62	      Blocking.group(2, "r2:wang|wei|-", 150, members.iterator.map(write(_)), rekeyed += _)
    63	    assert(size == 5000 && dupes == 0)
    64	    g match
    65	      case Blocking.Group.Oversize(r) => assert(r.size == 5000 && r.key == "r2:wang|wei|-")
    66	      case other                      => fail(s"expected an oversize report, got $other")
    67	    assert(rekeyed.isEmpty, "the last level re-keyed members")
    68	  }
    69	
    70	  test("a 5,000-member group below the last level is re-keyed in full, each member exactly once") {
    71	    val members = (1 to 5000).map(i => member(work(i, Seq("Wei Wang", s"Coauthor$i Family$i")), 1))
    72	    val rekeyed = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
    73	    val (g, size, _) =
    74	      Blocking.group(0, "r0:wang|w", 150, members.iterator.map(write(_)), rekeyed += _)
    75	    assert(g == Blocking.Group.Rekeyed(5000) && size == 5000)
    76	    val ids = rekeyed.map(j => upickle.default.read[BlockMember](j).id)
    77	    assert(ids.size == 5000 && ids.distinct.size == 5000)
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  test(
    81	    "duplicate member values before the overflow are de-duplicated and counted; a kept block is at most max"
    82	  ) {
    83	    val ms = (1 to 150).map(i => member(work(i, Seq("Ada Lovelace")), 1))
    84	    val values = (ms ++ ms.take(20)).iterator.map(write(_))
    85	    val (g, size, dupes) =
    86	      Blocking.group(0, "r0:lovelace|a", 150, values, _ => fail("re-keyed a block of 150"))
    87	    assert(size == 150 && dupes == 20)
    88	    g match
    89	      case Blocking.Group.Kept(b) =>
    90	        assert(b.members.size == 150 && b.members.map(_.id) == b.members.map(_.id).sorted)
    91	        val (pairs, sameWork) = Blocking.pairs(b)
    92	        val ps = pairs.toVector
    93	        assert(ps.size == 150 * 149 / 2 && sameWork == 0)
    94	        assert(ps.forall((a, c) => CanonicalPair.isCanonical(a.id, c.id)))
    95	      case other => fail(s"$other")
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  test(
    99	    "common names over three levels: every keyed mention ends in exactly one kept block or one oversize report"
   100	  ) {
   101	    // 3,000 "Wei Wang" mentions: 2,000 with one of 20 coauthor families, 1,000 single-author.
   102	    val mentions = (1 to 3000).map { i =>
   103	      val co = if i <= 2000 then Seq(s"Coauthor Fam${i % 20}") else Seq.empty
   104	      (s"m$i", BlockingKeys.researcher("Wei Wang", co))
   105	    }
   106	    val plan = OversizePolicy.plan[(String, Vector[Vector[String]])](
   107	      mentions,
   108	      _._1,
   109	      _._2,
   110	      BlockingKeys.ResearcherLevels,
   111	      150
   112	    )
   113	    assert(plan.blocks.forall(_.memberIds.size <= 150))
   114	    val inBlocks = plan.blocks.flatMap(_.memberIds).toSet
   115	    val inOversize = plan.oversize.map(_.size).sum
   116	    assert(plan.oversize.exists(_.key == "r2:wang|wei|-"), plan.oversize)
   117	    assert(
   118	      inBlocks.size == 2000 && inOversize == 1000,
   119	      s"${inBlocks.size} in blocks, $inOversize oversize"
   120	    )
   121	    assert(plan.missingKey.isEmpty)
   122	    // The same plan whatever the input order.
   123	    assert(
   124	      OversizePolicy.plan[(String, Vector[Vector[String]])](
   125	        mentions.reverse,
   126	        _._1,
   127	        _._2,
   128	        BlockingKeys.ResearcherLevels,
   129	        150
   130	      ) == plan
   131	    )
   132	  }
   133	
   134	  test(
   135	    "pair expansion: empty, solo, and duo teams; mentions of one work never pair inside a block"
   136	  ) {
   137	    assert(Teams.pairs(work(1, Seq())).isEmpty && Teams.pairCount(0) == 0)
   138	    assert(Teams.pairs(work(2, Seq("A B"))).isEmpty && Teams.pairCount(1) == 0)
   139	    val duo = Teams.pairs(work(3, Seq("Zed Z", "Ada A"))).toVector
   140	    assert(duo.size == 1 && CanonicalPair.isCanonical(duo.head.authorA, duo.head.authorB))
   141	    // A repeated name on one work is two positions and one pair; the work's own block never pairs them.
   142	    val twice = work(4, Seq("Wei Wang 0001", "Wei Wang 0001"))
   143	    assert(Teams.pairs(twice).size == 1)
   144	    val block = scholarchain.hadoop.BlockRecord(
   145	      Blocking.SchemaVersion,
   146	      MatchEntityType.RESEARCHER,
   147	      0,
   148	      "r0:wang|w",
   149	      Blocking.members(twice).tail.sortBy(_.id)
   150	    )
   151	    val (ps, sameWork) = Blocking.pairs(block)
   152	    assert(ps.isEmpty && sameWork == 1)
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  test("a 2,000-author team expands lazily into exactly n(n-1)/2 canonical, valid pairs") {
   156	    val n = 2000
   157	    val w = work(5, (1 to n).map(i => s"Author$i Family$i"), year = 2019)
   158	    assert(Teams.pairCount(n) == 1999000L)
   159	    var count = 0L
   160	    var maxPos = 0
   161	    Teams.pairs(w).foreach { p =>
   162	      count += 1
   163	      assert(CanonicalPair.isCanonical(p.authorA, p.authorB) && p.authorA != p.authorB)
   164	      if count % 99991 == 1 then assert(Validation.validate(p).isRight, p)
   165	      maxPos = math.max(maxPos, p.authorB.drop(p.authorB.lastIndexOf('#') + 1).toInt)
   166	    }
   167	    assert(count == Teams.pairCount(n) && maxPos == n)
   168	    assert(Teams.bucket(n) == "101+" && Validation.validate(Teams.team(w)).isRight)
   169	    // Overflow guard: the count is a Long for teams beyond 65,536 authors.
   170	    assert(Teams.pairCount(100000) == 4999950000L)
   171	  }

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/adversarial/scholarchain/tests/adversarial/CoauthorGraphEndpointAdversarialSpec.scala (174 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.adversarial
     2	
     3	import org.apache.spark.sql.functions.col
     4	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     5	import scholarchain.core.StableIds
     6	import scholarchain.core.config.ConfigLoader
     7	import scholarchain.spark.{CoauthorGraph, Ctx}
     8	import scholarchain.tests.support.SparkWorld.*
     9	import scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld
    10	
    11	/** Phase 11 work order 4 (ruling 12), spec category "coauthor edges with unresolved or conflicting
    12	  * researcher endpoints", on the production Spark job 2 code (CoauthorGraph.build and .integrity).
    13	  * A pair candidate whose mention or work has no canonical row is never an edge and is always a
    14	  * named violation; a pair whose two mentions resolved to one researcher is a counted self-pair,
    15	  * never an edge; merged works and reversed pairs never inflate a count.
    16	  */
    17	class CoauthorGraphEndpointAdversarialSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    18	  private val cfg = ConfigLoader.load(StoreWorld.baseEnv).fold(e => sys.error(e.mkString), identity)
    19	  private val windows = cfg.graph.windows
    20	  private val runId = StableIds.runId("adv-coauthor-graph", "c", "config-v1", Seq("g"), 1)
    21	  private val ctx = Ctx("2026-10-03", runId, "canon-v1.er-v2", "graph-v1", 50)
    22	  private def rid(s: String) = StoreWorld.rid(s)
    23	  private val (ra, rb, rc) = (rid("a"), rid("b"), rid("c"))
    24	
    25	  // m1 and m3 are one researcher (a merge); m2 is b; m4 is c. Source works w1 and w2 are one
    26	  // canonical work (a duplicate); w3 is another.
    27	  private val mentionMap = df(
    28	    "mentionId STRING, researcherId STRING",
    29	    Seq("m1", ra),
    30	    Seq("m2", rb),
    31	    Seq("m3", ra),
    32	    Seq("m4", rc),
    33	    Seq("m4", rc) // the same mention twice (a duplicated input row), same researcher
    34	  )
    35	  private val workMap = df(
    36	    "sourceWorkId STRING, workId STRING",
    37	    Seq("w1", "dblp:conf/x/C1"),
    38	    Seq("w2", "dblp:conf/x/C1"),
    39	    Seq("w3", "dblp:conf/x/C3")
    40	  )
    41	  private def pairs(rows: (String, String, String, Int)*) =
    42	    df(
    43	      "authorA STRING, authorB STRING, workId STRING, year INT",
    44	      rows.map((a, b, w, y) => Seq(a, b, w, y))*
    45	    )
    46	
    47	  private def build(rows: (String, String, String, Int)*) =
    48	    CoauthorGraph.build(ctx, spark, pairs(rows*), mentionMap, workMap, windows)
    49	
    50	  test("unresolved endpoints and missing works are violations, never edges") {
    51	    val out = build(
    52	      ("m1", "mX", "w1", 2005), // mX is in no mention map
    53	      ("mY", "m2", "w3", 2005), // mY too, on the other side
    54	      ("m4", "m2", "wX", 2005), // wX is in no work map
    55	      ("mY", "mZ", "wZ", 2005), // nothing resolves
    56	      ("m1", "m2", "w3", 2005) // the one good pair
    57	    )
    58	    val v = rows(out.violations, "check", "subjectId", "relatedId")
    59	      .map(x => (x(0).toString, x(1).toString, x(2).toString))
    60	      .toSet
    61	    assert(
    62	      v == Set(
    63	        ("PAIR_ENDPOINT_MISSING", "m1|mX", "w1"),
    64	        ("PAIR_ENDPOINT_MISSING", "mY|m2", "w3"),
    65	        ("PAIR_WORK_MISSING", "m4|m2", "wX"),
    66	        ("PAIR_ENDPOINT_MISSING", "mY|mZ", "wZ"),
    67	        ("PAIR_WORK_MISSING", "mY|mZ", "wZ")
    68	      ),
    69	      v
    70	    )
    71	    val e = rows(out.edges, "window", "researcherA", "researcherB", "jointPublicationCount")
    72	    val (lo, hi) = if ra < rb then (ra, rb) else (rb, ra)
    73	    assert(e.map(_.drop(1)).toSet == Set(Seq(lo, hi, 1L)), e)
    74	    // 2005 is inside all-years and 2000-2014, outside 2015-2026.
    75	    assert(e.map(_.head).toSet == Set("all-years", "2000-2014"), e)
    76	    assert(out.selfPairs.count() == 0 && out.outsideWindows.count() == 0)
    77	  }
    78	
    79	  test("a pair whose two mentions resolved to one researcher is a self-pair, never a self-edge") {
    80	    val out = build(("m1", "m3", "w3", 2010), ("m3", "m1", "w1", 2011))
    81	    assert(out.edges.count() == 0)
    82	    val self = rows(out.selfPairs, "authorA", "authorB", "researcherId")
    83	    assert(self.toSet == Set(Seq("m1", "m3", ra), Seq("m3", "m1", ra)), self)
    84	    // Provenance names the pair and the canonical policy.
    85	    val p = out.selfPairs.select(col("provenance.sourceRecordId"), col("provenance.policyVersion"))
    86	    assert(
    87	      rows(p, "sourceRecordId", "policyVersion").toSet ==
    88	        Set(Seq("m1|m3", "canon-v1.er-v2"), Seq("m3|m1", "canon-v1.er-v2"))
    89	    )
    90	    assert(out.violations.count() == 0)
    91	  }
    92	
    93	  test("merged works, reversed pairs, and duplicated mention rows never inflate a joint count") {
    94	    val out = build(
    95	      ("m1", "m2", "w1", 2003),
    96	      ("m2", "m1", "w2", 2003), // reversed, on the source work merged into the same canonical work
    97	      ("m3", "m2", "w3", 2016), // m3 is a's other mention: same researcher pair, another work
    98	      ("m4", "m2", "w3", 2016) // m4 appears twice in the mention map
    99	    )
   100	    val e = rows(
   101	      out.edges,
   102	      "window",
   103	      "researcherA",
   104	      "researcherB",
   105	      "jointPublicationCount",
   106	      "firstCollaborationYear",
   107	      "lastCollaborationYear",
   108	      "representativeWorkIds"
   109	    ).map(x => (x(0), Set(x(1), x(2))) -> x.drop(3)).toMap
   110	    val ab = Set[Any](ra, rb)
   111	    val cb = Set[Any](rc, rb)
   112	    assert(
   113	      e(("all-years", ab)).take(3) == Seq(2L, 2003, 2016) &&
   114	        e(("all-years", ab))(3).asInstanceOf[scala.collection.Seq[String]].toSeq ==
   115	        Seq("dblp:conf/x/C1", "dblp:conf/x/C3"),
   116	      e
   117	    )
   118	    assert(e(("2000-2014", ab)).take(3) == Seq(1L, 2003, 2003))
   119	    assert(e(("2015-2026", ab)).take(3) == Seq(1L, 2016, 2016))
   120	    assert(e(("all-years", cb)).head == 1L && e(("2015-2026", cb)).head == 1L)
   121	    assert(!e.contains(("2000-2014", cb)))
   122	    // Canonical order and stable IDs: researcherA < researcherB, edge ID from the core formula.
   123	    rows(out.edges, "researcherA", "researcherB", "windowStartYear", "windowEndYear", "edgeId")
   124	      .foreach { x =>
   125	        val (a, b) = (x(0).toString, x(1).toString)
   126	        assert(a < b, x)
   127	        val id =
   128	          StableIds.coauthorEdgeId(a, b, x(2).asInstanceOf[Int], x(3).asInstanceOf[Int], "graph-v1")
   129	        assert(id == Right(x(4)), x)
   130	      }
   131	    assert(out.violations.count() == 0 && out.selfPairs.count() == 0)
   132	  }
   133	
   134	  test("a pair outside every window is counted, not an edge; window bounds are inclusive") {
   135	    val out = build(
   136	      ("m1", "m2", "w1", 1999),
   137	      ("m1", "m2", "w3", 2027),
   138	      ("m1", "m4", "w3", 2000),
   139	      ("m2", "m4", "w3", 2026),
   140	      ("m1", "m2", "w3", 2014),
   141	      ("m1", "m2", "w1", 2015)
   142	    )
   143	    assert(out.outsideWindows.count() == 2)
   144	    val e = rows(out.edges, "window", "researcherA", "researcherB", "jointPublicationCount")
   145	      .map(x => (x(0), Set(x(1), x(2))) -> x(3))
   146	      .toMap
   147	    assert(e(("2000-2014", Set[Any](ra, rc))) == 1L && e(("2015-2026", Set[Any](rb, rc))) == 1L)
   148	    assert(e(("2000-2014", Set[Any](ra, rb))) == 1L && e(("2015-2026", Set[Any](ra, rb))) == 1L)
   149	    assert(e(("all-years", Set[Any](ra, rb))) == 2L)
   150	    assert(!e.keys.exists(k => k._1 == "2015-2026" && k._2 == Set[Any](ra, rc)))
   151	  }
   152	
   153	  test(
   154	    "integrity: an edge whose endpoint is not a canonical researcher is named, with its edge ID"
   155	  ) {
   156	    val out = build(("m1", "m2", "w3", 2005), ("m4", "m2", "w3", 2005))
   157	    // Only a and b are canonical researchers here; every edge touching c is a violation.
   158	    val researchers = df("researcherId STRING", Seq(ra), Seq(rb))
   159	    val v = rows(CoauthorGraph.integrity(out.edges, researchers), "check", "subjectId", "relatedId")

   161	      rows(out.edges.where(col("researcherA") === rc || col("researcherB") === rc), "edgeId")
   162	        .map(_.head.toString)
   163	        .toSet
   164	    assert(cEdges.size == 2, cEdges) // all-years and 2000-2014
   165	    assert(v.map(_.head).toSet == Set("COAUTHOR_EDGE_ENDPOINT_MISSING"))
   166	    assert(v.map(_(1).toString).toSet == cEdges, v)
   167	    assert(
   168	      CoauthorGraph
   169	        .integrity(out.edges, df("researcherId STRING", Seq(ra), Seq(rb), Seq(rc)))
   170	        .count() == 0
   171	    )
   172	    // Both ends missing: one violation per missing end.
   173	    assert(CoauthorGraph.integrity(out.edges, df("researcherId STRING", Seq(ra))).count() == 6)
   174	  }

     1	package scholarchain.tests.adversarial
     2	
     3	import org.apache.hadoop.conf.Configuration
     4	import org.apache.hadoop.fs.Path

    10	import scholarchain.hadoop.{DblpEntities, DblpInputFormat, DblpParser, DblpRecordReader, RunContext}

    17	/** Phase 11 work order 4 (ruling 12), spec category "malformed and truncated DBLP XML" at the
    18	  * Hadoop record reader (DblpInputFormat): truncation, split boundaries through the real input
    19	  * format, and record start tags that are not elements (inside an XML comment). Local `file:` paths
    20	  * in JVM temporary directories only; no mini-cluster and no network.
    21	  */
    22	class DblpRecordReaderAdversarialSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    23	  private val conf = Configuration()
    24	  private lazy val repo =
    25	    Iterator
    26	      .iterate(File(sys.props("user.dir")).getAbsoluteFile)(_.getParentFile)
    27	      .takeWhile(_ != null)
    28	      .find(d => File(d, "datasets/samples/dblp").isDirectory)
    29	      .get
    30	  private lazy val parser = DblpParser(
    31	    DblpEntities.parseDtd(
    32	      String(
    33	        Files.readAllBytes(File(repo, "datasets/samples/dblp/dblp-2023-06-28.dtd").toPath),
    34	        ISO_8859_1
    35	      )
    36	    )
    37	  )
    38	  private val ctx = RunContext(
    39	    "adv-j01",
    40	    StableIds.runId("adv", "code", "config-v1", Seq("dblp=2026-10-03"), 1),
    41	    Map(SourceType.DBLP -> "2026-10-03"),
    42	    "2026-10-03",
    43	    "graph-v1"
    44	  )
    45	
    46	  private def withFile[A](xml: String, suffix: String = ".xml")(body: (Path, Long) => A): A =
    47	    val dir = Files.createTempDirectory("adv-dblp-reader-")
    48	    try
    49	      val f = dir.resolve("dblp" + suffix)
    50	      if suffix.endsWith(".gz") then
    51	        val gz = java.util.zip.GZIPOutputStream(Files.newOutputStream(f))
    52	        try gz.write(xml.getBytes(ISO_8859_1))
    53	        finally gz.close()
    54	      else Files.write(f, xml.getBytes(ISO_8859_1))
    55	      body(Path(f.toUri), Files.size(f))
    56	    finally scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.deleteTree(dir)
    57	
    58	  private def read(file: Path, start: Long, length: Long): Vector[(Long, String)] =
    59	    val rr = DblpRecordReader()
    60	    rr.initialize(
    61	      FileSplit(file, start, length, Array.empty[String]),
    62	      TaskAttemptContextImpl(conf, TaskAttemptID())
    63	    )
    64	    try
    65	      Iterator
    66	        .continually(rr.nextKeyValue())
    67	        .takeWhile(identity)
    68	        .map(_ => (rr.getCurrentKey.get, rr.getCurrentValue.toString))
    69	        .toVector
    70	    finally rr.close()
    71	
    72	  /** Records from the splits DblpInputFormat itself makes with the given maximum split size. */
    73	  private def viaInputFormat(file: Path, maxSplit: Long): (Int, Vector[(Long, String)]) =
    74	    val job = Job.getInstance(conf)
    75	    FileInputFormat.setInputPaths(job, file)
    76	    FileInputFormat.setMaxInputSplitSize(job, maxSplit)
    77	    FileInputFormat.setMinInputSplitSize(job, 1L)
    78	    val splits = DblpInputFormat().getSplits(job).asScala.toVector.map(_.asInstanceOf[FileSplit])
    79	    (splits.size, splits.flatMap(s => read(file, s.getStart, s.getLength)))
    80	
    81	  private def keyOf(value: String) = """key="([^"]*)"""".r.findFirstMatchIn(value).map(_.group(1))
    82	  private def accepted(value: String) =
    83	    parser
    84	      .parse(value, ctx)
    85	      .toOption
    86	      .map(p => upickle.default.read[scholarchain.core.WorkRecord](p.work.json))
    87	
    88	  private val wellFormed =
    89	    """<?xml version="1.0" encoding="ISO-8859-1"?>
    90	      |<!DOCTYPE dblp SYSTEM "dblp.dtd">
    91	      |<dblp>
    92	      |<article key="journals/x/A1"><author>Ada Lovelace</author><title>One.</title><year>2001</year></article>
    93	      |<articles>not a record element</articles>
    94	      |<inproceedings key="conf/x/B2"><author>Grace Hopper</author><booktitle>B</booktitle><title>Two &amp; more.</title><year>2002</year></inproceedings>
    95	      |<article-x key="journals/x/NotOne"><title>no</title></article-x>
    96	      |<article	key="journals/x/T3"><title>Tab before the attribute, Jos&eacute; café.</title><year>2003</year></article>
    97	      |<www key="homepages/x/H"><title>Home</title></www>
    98	      |<article
    99	      |key="journals/x/N4"><title>Newline before the attribute.</title><year>2004</year></article>
   100	      |</dblp>
   101	      |""".stripMargin
   102	
   103	  test(
   104	    "splits made by DblpInputFormat at every size give every record once, intact, at its offset"
   105	  ) {
   106	    withFile(wellFormed) { (file, len) =>
   107	      val whole = read(file, 0, len)
   108	      assert(
   109	        whole.flatMap(r => keyOf(r._2)) ==
   110	          Vector("journals/x/A1", "conf/x/B2", "journals/x/T3", "homepages/x/H", "journals/x/N4")
   111	      )
   112	      // Keys are byte offsets of the record's '<' (ISO-8859-1: one byte per character).
   113	      whole.foreach((k, v) => assert(wellFormed.substring(k.toInt).startsWith(v), k))
   114	      assert(
   115	        whole.forall((_, v) =>
   116	          v.endsWith("</article>") || v.endsWith("</inproceedings>") || v.endsWith("</www>")
   117	        )
   118	      )
   119	      for size <- Seq(1L, 2L, 7L, 31L, 64L, 100L, 333L, len) do
   120	        val (n, recs) = viaInputFormat(file, size)
   121	        assert(recs == whole, s"max split size $size ($n splits)")
   122	      // Non-ASCII Latin-1 bytes survive the split reading as Unicode text.
   123	      assert(whole.exists(_._2.contains("café")))
   124	    }
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  test(
   128	    "a truncated file never loses or repeats the complete records before the cut, at any split"
   129	  ) {
   130	    val cuts = Vector(
   131	      wellFormed.indexOf("<www") + 3, // inside a start tag name
   132	      wellFormed.indexOf("</www>") + 4, // inside an end tag
   133	      wellFormed.indexOf("Newline") + 3, // inside the last record's text
   134	      wellFormed.indexOf("</dblp>") // only the closing root tag missing
   135	    )
   136	    cuts.foreach { cut =>
   137	      val xml = wellFormed.take(cut)
   138	      withFile(xml) { (file, len) =>
   139	        val whole = read(file, 0, len)
   140	        val complete = whole.filter((_, v) =>
   141	          v.endsWith("</article>") || v.endsWith("</inproceedings>") || v.endsWith("</www>")
   142	        )
   143	        val partial = whole.filterNot(complete.contains)
   144	        // At most one partial record, and it is the last one, returned for a visible reject.
   145	        assert(partial.size <= 1 && partial.forall(_ == whole.last), s"cut $cut: $partial")
   146	        partial.foreach((_, v) =>
   147	          assert(parser.parseXml(v).left.map(_.reason) == Left("malformed-record"), v)
   148	        )
   149	        for size <- Seq(1L, 5L, 64L) do
   150	          assert(viaInputFormat(file, size)._2 == whole, s"cut $cut, split size $size")
   151	      }
   152	    }
   153	    // Degenerate inputs: empty, root only, a split past the end, an empty split.
   154	    withFile("")((file, len) => assert(read(file, 0, len).isEmpty))
   155	    withFile("<dblp>\n</dblp>\n")((file, len) => assert(read(file, 0, len).isEmpty))
   156	    withFile(wellFormed) { (file, len) =>
   157	      assert(read(file, len, 100).isEmpty)
   158	      assert(read(file, 10, 0).isEmpty)
   159	    }
   160	  }
   161	
   162	  test("compressed input is one split and reads every record, whatever split it is handed") {
   163	    withFile(wellFormed, ".xml.gz") { (file, len) =>
   164	      val (n, recs) = viaInputFormat(file, 16L)
   165	      assert(n == 1, s"$n splits of a gzip file")
   166	      withFile(wellFormed)((plain, plen) => assert(recs == read(plain, 0, plen)))
   167	      // A reader handed a partial split of a compressed file still reads the whole stream once.
   168	      assert(read(file, 0, 10) == recs)
   169	    }
   170	  }
   171	
   172	  test(
   173	    "a record start tag inside an XML comment is not a record, and the records after it stay intact"
   174	  ) {
   175	    // PENDING D17 (owner hadoop-engineer): fails without a production fix; see tests/TESTS.md.
   176	    pendingUntilFixed {
   177	      // Found in work order 4 (D17). DblpInputFormat's contract: a value is a record element. Text
   178	      // inside <!-- ... --> is not an element, so a commented-out record must not become a work, and
   179	      // an unclosed record start inside a comment must not swallow the real records after it.
   180	      val xml =
   181	        """<?xml version="1.0" encoding="ISO-8859-1"?>
   182	          |<dblp>
   183	          |<!-- withdrawn: <article key="journals/x/Fake1"><author>Mallory</author><title>Fake.</title><year>2001</year></article> -->
   184	          |<article key="journals/x/Real1"><author>Ada Lovelace</author><title>Real one.</title><year>2001</year></article>
   185	          |<!-- <article key="journals/x/Open"> was never closed here -->
   186	          |<inproceedings key="conf/x/Real2"><author>Grace Hopper</author><booktitle>B</booktitle><title>Real two.</title><year>2002</year></inproceedings>
   187	          |<article key="journals/x/Real3"><author>Edsger Dijkstra</author><title>Real three.</title><year>2003</year></article>
   188	          |</dblp>
   189	          |""".stripMargin
   190	      withFile(xml) { (file, len) =>
   191	        val recs = read(file, 0, len)
   192	        val keys = recs.flatMap(r => keyOf(r._2))
   193	        val works = recs.flatMap(r => accepted(r._2)).map(_.workId)
   194	        assert(
   195	          keys == Vector("journals/x/Real1", "conf/x/Real2", "journals/x/Real3"),
   196	          s"records read: $keys; works accepted: $works"
   197	        )
   198	        assert(works.size == 3 && !works.exists(_.contains("Fake1")), works)
   199	      }
   200	    }
   201	  }

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/adversarial/scholarchain/tests/adversarial/DblpXmlAdversarialSpec.scala (227 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.adversarial
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.*
     5	import scholarchain.hadoop.{DblpEntities, DblpParser, RunContext}
     6	import upickle.default.read
     7	
     8	import java.io.File
     9	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1
    10	import java.nio.file.Files
    11	
    12	/** Phase 11 WO3, spec category "malformed and truncated DBLP XML", through the J01 record parser
    13	  * (pure). The Hadoop record reader (splits, truncation, comments) is
    14	  * DblpRecordReaderAdversarialSpec (work order 4). Every malformed record must become a typed
    15	  * reject, never an exception, a silent accept, a file read, or a network call.
    16	  */
    17	class DblpXmlAdversarialSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    18	  private lazy val repo =
    19	    Iterator
    20	      .iterate(new File(sys.props("user.dir")).getAbsoluteFile)(_.getParentFile)
    21	      .takeWhile(_ != null)
    22	      .find(d => new File(d, "datasets/samples/dblp").isDirectory)
    23	      .get
    24	  private lazy val entities = DblpEntities.parseDtd(
    25	    new String(
    26	      Files.readAllBytes(new File(repo, "datasets/samples/dblp/dblp-2023-06-28.dtd").toPath),
    27	      ISO_8859_1
    28	    )
    29	  )
    30	  private lazy val parser = DblpParser(entities)
    31	  private val ctx = RunContext(
    32	    "adv-j01",
    33	    StableIds.runId("adv", "code", "config-v1", Seq("dblp=2026-10-03"), 1),
    34	    Map(SourceType.DBLP -> "2026-10-03"),
    35	    "2026-10-03",
    36	    "graph-v1"
    37	  )
    38	  private def reason(xml: String): Either[String, WorkRecord] =
    39	    scala.util
    40	      .Try(parser.parse(xml, ctx))
    41	      .fold(
    42	        e => fail(s"the parser threw ${e.getClass.getSimpleName} instead of rejecting"),
    43	        _.left.map(_.reason).map(p => read[WorkRecord](p.work.json))
    44	      )
    45	
    46	  test("truncated, mismatched, and unclosed records are malformed-record rejects") {
    47	    Vector(
    48	      """<article key="journals/x/T1"><title>Truncated""",
    49	      """<article key="journals/x/T2"><title>A</title><author>B</author>""",
    50	      """<article key="journals/x/T3"><title>A</author></article>""",
    51	      """<article key="journals/x/T4"><title>A<title></article>""",
    52	      """<article key="journals/x/T5" <title>A</title></article>""",
    53	      """<article key=journals/x/T8><title>A</title></article>""",
    54	      """<article key="journals/x/T9"><title>a &amp b</title></article>""",
    55	      """<article key="journals/x/T10"><title>&#0;</title></article>""",
    56	      """<article key="journals/x/T11"><title>&#xD800;</title></article>""",
    57	      """<article key="journals/x/T12"><title>&#x110000;</title></article>""",
    58	      "",
    59	      "   ",
    60	      "<!-- only a comment -->"
    61	    ).foreach { xml =>
    62	      reason(xml) match
    63	        case Left(r) =>
    64	          assert(Set("malformed-record", "unknown-entity", "missing-key").contains(r), s"$r: $xml")
    65	        case Right(w) => fail(s"accepted ${w.workId}: $xml")
    66	    }
    67	  }
    68	
    69	  test(
    70	    "external entities and entity expansion bombs are refused; a missing external DTD changes nothing"
    71	  ) {
    72	    val secret = Files.createTempFile("adv-xxe-", ".txt")
    73	    try
    74	      Files.writeString(secret, "SYNTHETIC-SECRET-FILE-CONTENT")
    75	      val uri = secret.toUri.toString
    76	      val xxe =
    77	        s"""<!DOCTYPE article [<!ENTITY xxe SYSTEM "$uri">]><article key="journals/x/X1"><title>&xxe;</title></article>"""
    78	      assert(reason(xxe) == Left("unknown-entity"))
    79	      val bomb =
    80	        """<!DOCTYPE article [<!ENTITY a "lol"><!ENTITY b "&a;&a;&a;&a;&a;&a;&a;&a;"><!ENTITY c "&b;&b;&b;&b;&b;&b;&b;&b;">]>""" +
    81	          """<article key="journals/x/X2"><title>&c;</title></article>"""
    82	      assert(reason(bomb) == Left("unknown-entity"))
    83	      // A DOCTYPE that names an external DTD is ignored, not loaded: a missing file changes nothing.
    84	      val missingDtd =
    85	        """<!DOCTYPE article SYSTEM "file:///nonexistent/adv/dblp.dtd"><article key="journals/x/X3"><title>Fine</title></article>"""
    86	      assert(reason(missingDtd).map(_.title) == Right(Some("Fine")))
    87	      // A parameter entity referencing the secret file is never expanded (that nothing is even
    88	      // fetched is shown by the fetch-counting test below).
    89	      val pe =
    90	        s"""<!DOCTYPE article [<!ENTITY % p SYSTEM "$uri"> %p;]><article key="journals/x/X4"><title>T</title></article>"""
    91	      reason(pe) match
    92	        case Right(w) => assert(!w.title.exists(_.contains("SYNTHETIC-SECRET")))
    93	        case Left(_)  => ()
    94	    finally Files.deleteIfExists(secret)
    95	  }
    96	
    97	  test("no external entity, parameter entity, or DTD reference is ever fetched (fetches counted)") {
    98	    // Work order 4 (code review 1 section 5): a Left alone does not prove that nothing was read.
    99	    // Every reference points at a loopback server (127.0.0.1, ephemeral port) that counts requests;
   100	    // a parser that resolves any of them fetches it before it can fail.
   101	    val hits = java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger()
   102	    val server = com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer.create(
   103	      java.net.InetSocketAddress(java.net.InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress, 0),
   104	      0
   105	    )
   106	    server.createContext(
   107	      "/",
   108	      ex =>
   109	        hits.incrementAndGet()
   110	        val body = """<!ENTITY % q "<!ENTITY leak 'SYNTHETIC-SECRET'>"> %q;""".getBytes("UTF-8")
   111	        ex.sendResponseHeaders(200, body.length.toLong)
   112	        ex.getResponseBody.write(body)
   113	        ex.close()
   114	    )
   115	    server.start()
   116	    try
   117	      val base = s"http://127.0.0.1:${server.getAddress.getPort}"
   118	      // Control: the counter sees a real fetch.
   119	      val in = java.net.URI(s"$base/control").toURL.openStream()
   120	      try in.readAllBytes()
   121	      finally in.close()
   122	      assert(hits.get == 1, "the fetch counter does not work")
   123	      hits.set(0)
   124	      val docs = Vector(
   125	        s"""<!DOCTYPE article [<!ENTITY xxe SYSTEM "$base/general">]><article key="journals/x/F1"><title>&xxe;</title></article>""",
   126	        s"""<!DOCTYPE article [<!ENTITY % p SYSTEM "$base/parameter"> %p;]><article key="journals/x/F2"><title>T</title></article>""",
   127	        s"""<!DOCTYPE article [<!ENTITY % p SYSTEM "$base/nested"> %p; %q;]><article key="journals/x/F3"><title>&leak;</title></article>""",
   128	        s"""<!DOCTYPE article SYSTEM "$base/external.dtd"><article key="journals/x/F4"><title>Fine</title></article>""",
   129	        s"""<!DOCTYPE article PUBLIC "-//X//Y//EN" "$base/public.dtd"><article key="journals/x/F5"><title>Fine</title></article>""",
   130	        s"""<?xml-stylesheet href="$base/style.xsl"?><article key="journals/x/F6"><title>Fine</title></article>""",
   131	        s"""<article key="journals/x/F7" xmlns:xi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XInclude"><title><xi:include href="$base/xinclude"/></title></article>"""
   132	      )
   133	      docs.foreach { xml =>
   134	        val r = reason(xml)
   135	        assert(hits.get == 0, s"fetched while parsing: $xml")
   136	        r.foreach(w => assert(!w.title.exists(_.contains("SYNTHETIC-SECRET")), xml))
   137	      }
   138	      // The documents that need no external text still parse to their own title.
   139	      assert(reason(docs(3)).map(_.title) == Right(Some("Fine")))
   140	      assert(reason(docs(4)).map(_.title) == Right(Some("Fine")))
   141	    finally server.stop(0)
   142	  }
   143	
   144	  test("missing or blank keys, unsupported types, and malformed years are typed rejects") {
   145	    assert(reason("""<article><title>A</title></article>""") == Left("missing-key"))
   146	    assert(reason("""<article key="   "><title>A</title></article>""") == Left("missing-key"))
   147	    assert(
   148	      reason("""<www key="homepages/x/1"><title>A</title></www>""") == Left(
   149	        "unsupported-record-type"
   150	      )
   151	    )
   152	    assert(
   153	      reason("""<proceedings key="conf/x/2020"><title>A</title></proceedings>""") == Left(
   154	        "unsupported-record-type"
   155	      )
   156	    )
   157	    Vector("2O20", "20 20", "MMXX", "2020.0", "").foreach { y =>
   158	      val r = reason(s"""<article key="journals/x/Y"><title>A</title><year>$y</year></article>""")
   159	      assert(
   160	        r.left.toOption.contains("schema-validation") || (y.isEmpty && r.isRight),

   134	  test("a Neptune read that never answers is cut at services.timeouts.neptune-ms (504)") {
   135	    withWorld("services.timeouts.neptune-ms = 300\nneptune.query-timeout-ms = 200") { w =>
   136	      val entered = CountDownLatch(1)
   137	      val release = CountDownLatch(1)
   138	      val graph = ScriptedGraph(
   139	        GraphReadLimits.from(w.cfg),
   140	        id =>
   141	          if id != "probe:readiness" then
   142	            entered.countDown()
   143	            release.await(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
   144	      )
   145	      val b = w.backend.copy(ports = w.backend.ports.copy(graph = graph))
   146	      val log = CaptureLog()
   147	      try
   148	        w.graphEvidence(log.log, b) { c =>
   149	          val r = c.get(s"/v1/graph/nodes/${enc(graph.known)}")
   150	          // The read is still held (release is counted down only in finally), so this 504 is the
   151	          // neptune-ms timeout, not a store answer.
   152	          assert(r.status == 504 && r.error == "DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT", r.text)
   153	          assert(release.getCount == 1)
   154	          // Work order 4 (finding 4): the timeout may fire before the read thread is scheduled, so
   155	          // the read is awaited rather than assumed started; the 30 s bound is a hang guard only.
   156	          assert(entered.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS), "the graph read never started")
   157	          // The held call does not block other routes or readiness.
   158	          assert(c.get("/healthz", Seq()).status == 200)
   159	          assert(c.get(s"/v1/evidence/ev-${"a" * 32}").status == 404)
   160	        }
   161	      finally release.countDown()
   162	    }
   163	  }

   165	  test("the sync query deadline answers 504 while a store read is held; no breaker opens") {
   166	    withWorld(
   167	      "operations.synchronous-deadline-ms = 300\nservices.resilience.breaker-failure-threshold = 1"
   168	    ) { w =>
   169	      val entered = CountDownLatch(1)
   170	      val release = CountDownLatch(1)
   171	      val kv = new KeyValueReadPort:
   172	        def get(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String): Option[Item] =
   173	          entered.countDown(); release.await(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
   174	          w.backend.ports.kv.get(t, pk, sk)
   175	        def query(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String, limit: Int): Vector[Item] =
   176	          entered.countDown(); release.await(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
   177	          w.backend.ports.kv.query(t, pk, sk, limit)
   178	      val b = w.backend.copy(ports = w.backend.ports.copy(kv = kv))
   179	      val log = CaptureLog()
   180	      val m = Metrics()
   181	      try
   182	        w.query(log.log, b, m) { c =>
   183	          val r = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson())
   184	          // No store read has returned (release is counted down only in finally), so the 504 is
   185	          // the synchronous deadline.
   186	          assert(r.status == 504 && r.error == "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED", r.text)
   187	          assert(release.getCount == 1)
   188	          // Work order 4 (finding 4): on a cold JVM the deadline can pass before the workflow
   189	          // reaches its first key-value read; the workflow goes on after the 504 (workflows are not
   190	          // interrupted), so the read is awaited, never assumed. 30 s is a hang guard only.
   191	          assert(entered.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS), "the workflow never reached a store read")
   192	          assert(!m.snapshot("counters").obj.keys.exists(_.startsWith("circuitOpened")))
   193	          assert(c.get("/readyz", Seq()).status == 200)
   194	        }
   195	      finally release.countDown()
   196	    }

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/adversarial/scholarchain/tests/adversarial/IdempotencyRaceAdversarialSpec.scala (264 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.adversarial
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.JobState
     5	import scholarchain.services.*
     6	import scholarchain.storage.*
     7	import scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.*
     8	
     9	import java.time.Instant
    10	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.{AtomicBoolean, AtomicInteger}
    11	import java.util.concurrent.{Callable, CountDownLatch, Executors, TimeUnit}
    12	import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*
    13	
    14	/** Phase 11 WO3, ruling 1 target "idempotency races" and spec category "replayed workflow and
    15	  * idempotency". Concurrency is ordered with latches: a held workflow stays held until the test
    16	  * releases it, so no assertion depends on timing.
    17	  */
    18	class IdempotencyRaceAdversarialSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    19	
    20	  /** b whose first key-value read blocks until release (entered counts down when it blocks), and
    21	    * whose audit writer counts writes (one per workflow run).
    22	    */
    23	  private def held(w: World, entered: CountDownLatch, release: CountDownLatch) =
    24	    val first = AtomicBoolean(true)
    25	    val audits = AtomicInteger()
    26	    val b = w.backend
    27	    val kv = new KeyValueReadPort:
    28	      private def gate(): Unit =
    29	        if first.compareAndSet(true, false) then
    30	          entered.countDown()
    31	          release.await(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
    32	      def get(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String): Option[Item] = {
    33	        gate(); b.ports.kv.get(t, pk, sk)
    34	      }
    35	      def query(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String, limit: Int): Vector[Item] =
    36	        gate(); b.ports.kv.query(t, pk, sk, limit)
    37	    val writer = new ObjectLoadPort:
    38	      def putNew(key: String, bytes: Array[Byte]) =
    39	        if key.startsWith("logs/") && key.endsWith(".json") && !key.contains("/evidence/") then
    40	          audits.incrementAndGet()
    41	        b.ports.auditWriter.putNew(key, bytes)
    42	    (b.copy(ports = b.ports.copy(kv = kv, auditWriter = writer)), audits)
    43	
    44	  test("same key and body while the first sync query runs: 409, one workflow, then the replay") {
    45	    withWorld() { w =>
    46	      val log = CaptureLog()
    47	      val entered = CountDownLatch(1)
    48	      val release = CountDownLatch(1)
    49	      val (b, audits) = held(w, entered, release)
    50	      w.query(log.log, b) { c =>
    51	        val pool = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
    52	        try
    53	          val key = Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "race-sync-1")
    54	          val first = pool.submit(new Callable[Reply] {
    55	            def call() = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), key)
    56	          })
    57	          assert(entered.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS), "the first workflow never started")
    58	          // While the first runs: the same body is "still running", a different body a conflict.
    59	          (1 to 5).foreach { _ =>
    60	            val r = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), key)
    61	            assert(r.status == 409 && r.error == "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", r.text)
    62	            assert(r.body("message").str.contains("no stored answer"), r.text)
    63	          }
    64	          val other = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson("Who is Grace Hopper?"), key)
    65	          assert(
    66	            other.status == 409 && other.body("message").str.contains("different request body")
    67	          )
    68	          release.countDown()
    69	          val r1 = first.get(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
    70	          assert(r1.status == 200, r1.text)
    71	          val replay = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), key)
    72	          assert(replay.status == 200 && replay.headers.get("Idempotent-Replay").contains("true"))
    73	          assert(replay.body == r1.body)
    74	          assert(audits.get == 1, s"${audits.get} workflow runs for one key")
    75	        finally
    76	          release.countDown()
    77	          pool.shutdownNow()
    78	      }
    79	    }
    80	  }
    81	
    82	  test("eight concurrent identical keyed POSTs run one workflow and share one query ID") {
    83	    withWorld() { w =>
    84	      val log = CaptureLog()
    85	      val (b, audits) = held(w, CountDownLatch(1), CountDownLatch(0)) // never blocks
    86	      w.query(log.log, b) { c =>
    87	        val start = CountDownLatch(1)
    88	        val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(8)
    89	        try
    90	          val fs = (1 to 8).map(_ =>
    91	            pool.submit(new Callable[Reply] {
    92	              def call() =
    93	                start.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
    94	                c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "race-sync-8"))
    95	            })
    96	          )
    97	          start.countDown()
    98	          val rs = fs.map(_.get(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
    99	          assert(rs.forall(r => r.status == 200 || r.status == 409), rs.map(_.status))
   100	          val oks = rs.filter(_.status == 200)
   101	          assert(
   102	            oks.count(_.headers.get("Idempotent-Replay").isEmpty) == 1,
   103	            "not exactly one original"
   104	          )
   105	          assert(oks.map(_.body("queryId").str).distinct.size == 1)
   106	          assert(rs.filter(_.status == 409).forall(_.error == "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT"))
   107	          assert(audits.get == 1)
   108	        finally pool.shutdownNow()
   109	      }
   110	    }
   111	  }

   113	  test("async: a racing replay before the job exists is 409; afterwards one job and one message") {
   114	    withWorld() { w =>
   115	      val log = CaptureLog()
   116	      val entered = CountDownLatch(1)
   117	      val release = CountDownLatch(1)
   118	      val table = LocalServiceStateTable(None, () => Instant.now)
   119	      // Job creation blocks (after the key claim), so a second request sees the claim but no job.
   120	      val blocking = new ServiceStateTable:
   121	        def get(k: String, t: String) = table.get(k, t)
   122	        def putIfAbsent(item: Item) =
   123	          if item.get("recordType").contains(AttributeValue.S(ServiceStateContract.JobType)) then
   124	            entered.countDown()
   125	            release.await(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
   126	          table.putIfAbsent(item)
   127	        def putIfState(item: Item, expected: JobState) = table.putIfState(item, expected)
   128	      val q = InMemoryJobQueue(w.cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount)
   129	      val b = w.backend.copy(
   130	        idempotency =
   131	          IdempotencyStore(blocking, w.cfg.services.ttl.idempotencyHours, () => Instant.now),
   132	        jobs = JobStore(blocking, w.cfg.services.ttl.jobHours, () => Instant.now),
   133	        queue = q
   134	      )
   135	      w.query(log.log, b) { c =>
   136	        val pool = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
   137	        try
   138	          val key = Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "race-async-1")
   139	          val body = queryJson(extra = ", \"execution\": \"async\"")
   140	          val first = pool.submit(new Callable[Reply] {
   141	            def call() = c.post("/v1/query", body, key)
   142	          })
   143	          assert(entered.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   144	          val racing = c.post("/v1/query", body, key)
   145	          assert(racing.status == 409 && racing.error == "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", racing.text)
   146	          release.countDown()
   147	          val r1 = first.get(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
   148	          assert(r1.status == 202, r1.text)
   149	          val again = c.post("/v1/query", body, key)
   150	          assert(again.status == 202 && again.headers.get("Idempotent-Replay").contains("true"))
   151	          assert(again.body("jobId") == r1.body("jobId"))
   152	          assert(q.size == 1, s"${q.size} messages for one keyed async query")
   153	          // The same key on a sync body is a different request: 409, no second job.
   154	          val sync = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), key)
   155	          assert(sync.status == 409 && sync.body("message").str.contains("different request body"))
   156	          assert(q.size == 1)
   157	        finally
   158	          release.countDown()
   159	          pool.shutdownNow()
   160	      }
   161	    }
   162	  }

   164	  test(
   165	    "N3 (documented): a keyed sync POST answered 503 OVERLOADED keeps its key; the retry is 409"
   166	  ) {
   167	    // SERVICES.md section 3 and openapi.yaml (Phase 11 WO1 N3): the claim is not released when the
   168	    // workflow cannot start (deferred by Phase 10 ruling 18). This pins the documented behaviour.
   169	    withWorld("services.query-api.threads = 2") { w =>
   170	      val log = CaptureLog()
   171	      val entered = CountDownLatch(1)
   172	      val release = CountDownLatch(1)
   173	      val (b, _) = held(w, entered, release)
   174	      w.query(log.log, b) { c =>
   175	        val pool = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
   176	        try
   177	          // The one workflow slot (max(1, 2 / 2)) is held by an unkeyed query.
   178	          val holder = pool.submit(new Callable[Reply] {
   179	            def call() = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson())
   180	          })
   181	          assert(entered.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   182	          val key = Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "n3-overloaded")
   183	          val busy = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson("Who is Grace Hopper?"), key)
   184	          assert(
   185	            busy.status == 503 && busy.error == "OVERLOADED" && busy.body("retryable").bool,
   186	            busy.text
   187	          )
   188	          release.countDown()
   189	          assert(holder.get(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS).status == 200)
   190	          val retry = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson("Who is Grace Hopper?"), key)
   191	          assert(retry.status == 409 && retry.error == "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", retry.text)
   192	          // A new key goes through.
   193	          assert(
   194	            c.post(
   195	              "/v1/query",
   196	              queryJson("Who is Grace Hopper?"),
   197	              Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "n3-new")
   198	            ).status == 200
   199	          )
   200	        finally
   201	          release.countDown()
   202	          pool.shutdownNow()
   203	      }
   204	    }
   205	  }
   206	
   207	  test("an expired Idempotency-Key is a new request; the stored record holds only the key hash") {
   208	    var now = Instant.parse("2026-11-12T00:00:00Z")
   209	    val table = LocalServiceStateTable(None, () => now)
   210	    val store = IdempotencyStore(table, 24, () => now)
   211	    val q1 = "qry-" + "1" * 32
   212	    val q2 = "qry-" + "2" * 32
   213	    val h1 = "sha256:" + "a" * 64
   214	    val h2 = "sha256:" + "b" * 64
   215	    assert(
   216	      store.claim("client-key-secret-value", h1, q1).map(_.outcome) == Right(LoadOutcome.Inserted)
   217	    )
   218	    assert(
   219	      store.claim("client-key-secret-value", h1, q2).map(_.outcome) == Right(LoadOutcome.Unchanged)
   220	    )
   221	    assert(
   222	      store.claim("client-key-secret-value", h2, q2).map(_.outcome) == Right(LoadOutcome.Conflict)
   223	    )
   224	    // The stored item never holds the raw header value.
   225	    val stored = table.get(
   226	      ServiceStateContract.idempotencyRecordKey("client-key-secret-value"),
   227	      ServiceStateContract.IdempotencyType
   228	    )
   229	    assert(stored.exists(i => !i.values.exists(_.toString.contains("client-key-secret-value"))))
   230	    now = now.plusSeconds(24 * 3600L + 1)
   231	    val again = store.claim("client-key-secret-value", h2, q2)
   232	    assert(again.map(s => (s.outcome, s.record.queryId)) == Right((LoadOutcome.Inserted, q2)))
   233	  }
   234	
   235	  test("job transitions are linearizable: of 16 racing QUEUED -> RUNNING claims exactly one wins") {
   236	    val table = LocalServiceStateTable(None, () => Instant.now)
   237	    val jobs = JobStore(table, 168, () => Instant.now)
   238	    val qid = "qry-" + "c" * 32
   239	    assert(jobs.create(qid, "{\"question\":\"q\"}").isRight)
   240	    val jid = scholarchain.core.StableIds.jobId(qid)
   241	    val start = CountDownLatch(1)
   242	    val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(16)
   243	    try
   244	      val fs = (1 to 16).map(_ =>
   245	        pool.submit(new Callable[Either[String, JobTransition]] {
   246	          def call() =
   247	            start.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
   248	            jobs.transition(jid, JobState.QUEUED, JobState.RUNNING)
   249	        })
   250	      )
   251	      start.countDown()
   252	      val rs = fs.map(_.get(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   253	      assert(rs.count { case Right(JobTransition.Moved(_)) => true; case _ => false } == 1)
   254	      assert(rs.count {
   255	        case Right(JobTransition.Refused(Some(j))) => j.state == JobState.RUNNING; case _ => false
   256	      } == 15)
   257	      // No path back: a finished job can never be claimed again.
   258	      assert(jobs.transition(jid, JobState.RUNNING, JobState.SUCCEEDED).isRight)
   259	      assert(jobs.transition(jid, JobState.SUCCEEDED, JobState.RUNNING).isLeft)
   260	      assert(jobs.transition(jid, JobState.QUEUED, JobState.RUNNING) match
   261	        case Right(JobTransition.Refused(Some(j))) => j.state == JobState.SUCCEEDED
   262	        case _                                     => false)
   263	    finally pool.shutdownNow()
   264	  }

    63	  private def researcherVerdict(a: WorkRecord, ia: Int, b: WorkRecord, ib: Int) =
    64	    MatchPolicy.decideBlocked(
    65	      MatchEntityType.RESEARCHER,

    67	      t,
    68	      v2

    75	  test(
    76	    "canonically and compatibly equivalent spellings give one comparable name and one blocking key"
    77	  ) {
    78	    val forms = Vector(
    79	      "José Müller",
    80	      Normalizer.normalize("José Müller", Normalizer.Form.NFD),
    81	      "José Müller",
    82	      "ＪＯＳＥ ＭＵＬＬＥＲ", // fullwidth
    83	      "Müller, José",
    84	      "J̶o̷s̸é M̵ül̶l̷e̸r" // stacked combining marks
    85	    )
    86	    val base = Normalize.name(forms.head)
    87	    forms.foreach { f =>
    88	      assert(Normalize.name(f).comparable == base.comparable, f)
    89	      assert(keys(f) == keys(forms.head), f)
    90	    }
    91	    // Through the DBLP parser: an entity, a numeric reference, and the raw character agree.
    92	    val a = work("journals/x/U1", Seq("Jos&eacute; M&uuml;ller")).authors.head
    93	    val b = work("journals/x/U2", Seq("Jos&#233; M&#252;ller")).authors.head
    94	    val c = work("journals/x/U3", Seq("José Müller")).authors.head
    95	    assert(Set(a, b, c).map(_.normalizedName) == Set(base.comparable))
    96	    assert(Normalize.title("<i>Deep</i> Learning—ﬁne") == Normalize.title("Deep Learning fine"))
    97	  }

   298	    "exact DOI groups: cross-source duplicates match DBLP; a within-source duplicate is a conflict, never a merge"
   299	  ) {

   301	    val key = "doi:10.1145/abc.123"
   302	    val ok = ExactIdentifiers.resolve(
   303	      key,

   307	        e("doi:10.1145/abc.123", SourceType.CROSSREF),

   310	      ctx,
   311	      100,
   312	      v2
   313	    )
   314	    assert(
   315	      ok.matches.map(m => (m.sourceId, m.targetId)).toSet == Set(
   316	        "openalex:W1" -> "dblp:a/1",

   318	      )

   329	      100,
   330	      v2
   331	    )
   332	    assert(twoDblp.matches.isEmpty && twoDblp.conflicts.size == 1)
   333	    val decided = MatchPolicy.decide(twoDblp.conflicts.head, CandidateOrigin.EXACT_CONFLICT, t, v2)
   334	    assert(decided.outcome == MatchOutcome.CONFLICT)
   335	    // Above the conflict cap: no pairs, reported as oversize, and still nothing matches.
   336	    val many =

   338	    val capped = ExactIdentifiers.resolve(key, many, ctx, 100, v2)
   339	    assert(
   340	      capped.matches.isEmpty && capped.conflicts.isEmpty && capped.oversizeConflicts.map(
   341	        _.size

   348	  ) {
   349	    val a =
   350	      work("journals/x/D1", Seq("Ada Lovelace", "Grace Hopper"), ee = Seq("https://doi.org/10.1/a"))
   351	    val b =
   352	      work("journals/x/D2", Seq("Ada Lovelace", "Grace Hopper"), ee = Seq("https://doi.org/10.1/b"))
   353	        .copy(source = SourceType.OPENALEX, workId = "openalex:W2")
   354	    val v = MatchPolicy.decideBlocked(
   355	      MatchEntityType.WORK,
   356	      Features.work(a, None, b, None) ++ WorkPairFeatures.work(a, b, v2),
   357	      t,
   358	      v2
   359	    )
   360	    assert(v.outcome == MatchOutcome.CONFLICT, v)
   361	    assert(v.reasonCodes.contains("BIBLIOGRAPHIC_EVIDENCE_CONTRADICTS_ID"))
   362	    // Two DOIs on one DBLP record: the first is the work's DOI, the second stays in externalIds.
   363	    val two = work(
   364	      "journals/x/D3",
   365	      Seq("Ada Lovelace"),
   366	      ee = Seq("https://doi.org/10.1/first", "https://doi.org/10.1/second")
   367	    )
   368	    assert(
   369	      two.doi.contains("10.1/first") && two.externalIds.values.exists(_.endsWith("10.1/second"))
   370	    )
   371	  }

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/adversarial/scholarchain/tests/adversarial/LoggingRedactionAdversarialSpec.scala (166 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.adversarial
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.StableIds
     5	import scholarchain.services.*
     6	import scholarchain.storage.*
     7	import scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.*
     8	
     9	import java.net.URLEncoder
    10	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
    11	import java.time.Instant
    12	
    13	/** Phase 11 WO3, spec category "logging redaction" (Phase 10 ruling 12: one JSON line per request
    14	  * and per stage; never bodies, questions, keys, or retrieved text). Every input channel carries a
    15	  * SYNTHETIC marker; no log line and no error body may carry one back.
    16	  */
    17	class LoggingRedactionAdversarialSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    18	  private val Marker = "SYNTHETICSECRET"
    19	  private val RequestFields =
    20	    Set(
    21	      "ts",
    22	      "type",
    23	      "service",
    24	      "traceId",
    25	      "requestId",
    26	      "method",
    27	      "route",
    28	      "status",
    29	      "latencyMs",
    30	      "error"
    31	    )
    32	  private val StageFields =
    33	    Set("ts", "type", "traceId", "requestId", "queryId", "jobId", "stage", "outcome", "latencyMs")
    34	
    35	  private def failingKv(w: World) =
    36	    w.backend.copy(ports = w.backend.ports.copy(kv = new KeyValueReadPort:
    37	      def get(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String): Option[Item] =
    38	        throw IllegalStateException(s"$Marker-store-error endpoint=$Marker.internal")
    39	      def query(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String, limit: Int): Vector[Item] =
    40	        throw IllegalStateException(s"$Marker-store-error endpoint=$Marker.internal")))
    41	
    42	  test("markers in the question, headers, query string, path, and body never reach a log line") {
    43	    withWorld() { w =>
    44	      val log = CaptureLog()
    45	      val seen = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
    46	      w.query(log.log) { c =>
    47	        // Error bodies and headers; an answer may name a person the caller's own question named.
    48	        def keep(r: Reply) =
    49	          if r.status >= 400 then seen += r.text
    50	          seen ++= r.headers.values
    51	          r
    52	        keep(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(s"Who is Ada Lovelace $Marker?")))
    53	        keep(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(s"Who is $Marker?", ", \"execution\": \"async\"")))
    54	        keep(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> s"$Marker-idem")))
    55	        keep(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), Seq("X-Api-Key" -> s"$Marker-key")))
    56	        keep(c.post("/v1/query", s"""{"question": "$Marker", "$Marker": 1}"""))
    57	        keep(c.post("/v1/query", s"""{"question": "$Marker" """))
    58	        keep(
    59	          c.post("/v1/query", s"""{"question": "q", "analysis": {"rankingMetric": "$Marker"}}""")
    60	        )
    61	        keep(
    62	          c.post(
    63	            "/v1/query",
    64	            s"""{"question": "q", "constraints": {"topics": ["$Marker"], "$Marker": 1}}"""
    65	          )
    66	        )
    67	        keep(c.get(s"/v1/explain/qry-${"0" * 32}?$Marker=$Marker"))
    68	        keep(c.get(s"/v1/explain/$Marker"))
    69	        keep(c.get(s"/v1/jobs/${URLEncoder.encode(s"job-$Marker", UTF_8)}"))
    70	        keep(c.get(s"/v1/$Marker/x"))
    71	        keep(
    72	          c.get("/healthz", Seq("User-Agent" -> Marker, "X-Request-Id" -> s"$Marker with space"))
    73	        )
    74	        keep(c.get("/metrics", Seq("X-Api-Key" -> ApiKey, "Referer" -> Marker)))
    75	      }
    76	      w.query(log.log, failingKv(w)) { c =>
    77	        val r = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson())
    78	        assert(r.status == 502, r.text)
    79	        seen += r.text
    80	      }
    81	      val lines = log.all
    82	      assert(lines.nonEmpty)
    83	      lines.foreach { l =>
    84	        val j = scala.util.Try(ujson.read(l)).getOrElse(fail(s"not JSON: $l"))
    85	        val keys = j.obj.keySet.toSet
    86	        j("type").str match
    87	          case "request" => assert(keys == RequestFields, l)
    88	          case "stage"   => assert(keys.subsetOf(StageFields), l)
    89	          case "event"   => ()
    90	          case other     => fail(s"unexpected log type $other")
    91	        assert(!l.contains(Marker), s"log line carries a marker: $l")
    92	      }
    93	      // Routes are logged by template, never by raw path.
    94	      assert(
    95	        lines
    96	          .filter(_.contains("\"type\":\"request\""))
    97	          .forall(l =>
    98	            Set(
    99	              "/v1/query",
   100	              "/v1/explain/{queryId}",
   101	              "/v1/jobs/{jobId}",
   102	              "unmatched",
   103	              "/healthz",
   104	              "/metrics"
   105	            )
   106	              .contains(ujson.read(l)("route").str)
   107	          )
   108	      )
   109	      assert(!seen.exists(_.contains(Marker)), "a response echoes a marker")
   110	      assert(!seen.exists(_.contains(ApiKey)), "a response holds the API key")
   111	    }
   112	  }
   113	
   114	  test("worker events and job errors carry IDs and codes only, never the question") {
   115	    withWorld("services.worker.poll-wait-seconds = 1") { w =>
   116	      val log = CaptureLog()
   117	      val q = InMemoryJobQueue(w.cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount)
   118	      val b = w.backend.copy(queue = q)
   119	      val wk = Worker(w.cfg, b, log.log, Metrics())
   120	      try
   121	        def submit(question: String, backend: Backend) =
   122	          val qid =
   123	            StableIds.queryId(backend.setup.runId, StableIds.textHash(question), Ids.fresh("t"), 1)
   124	          assert(backend.jobs.create(qid, upickle.default.write(QueryBody(question))).isRight)
   125	          backend.queue.send(JobMessage.of(qid))
   126	          StableIds.jobId(qid)
   127	        val ok = submit(s"Have Ada Lovelace and Grace Hopper coauthored a paper $Marker?", b)
   128	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1)
   129	        // A failing store: the job fails with a typed error that holds no store message either.
   130	        val bad = b.copy(ports = failingKv(w).ports)
   131	        val wk2 = Worker(w.cfg, bad, log.log, Metrics())
   132	        try
   133	          val failed =
   134	            submit(s"Have Ada Lovelace and Grace Hopper coauthored a paper $Marker?", bad)
   135	          assert(wk2.pollOnce() == 1)
   136	          val j = bad.jobs.get(failed).toOption.flatten.get
   137	          assert(j.state == scholarchain.core.JobState.FAILED, j)
   138	          assert(j.error.exists(e => !e.message.contains(Marker)), j)
   139	        finally wk2.stop()
   140	        assert(
   141	          log.all.nonEmpty && !log.all.exists(_.contains(Marker)),
   142	          log.all.filter(_.contains(Marker))
   143	        )
   144	        assert(b.jobs.get(ok).toOption.flatten.exists(_.error.isEmpty))
   145	      finally wk.stop()
   146	    }
   147	  }
   148	
   149	  test("explain never returns question, prompt, retrieved, or model text") {
   150	    withWorld() { w =>
   151	      val log = CaptureLog()
   152	      w.query(log.log) { c =>
   153	        val m = Marker.toLowerCase
   154	        val r = c.post(
   155	          "/v1/query",
   156	          queryJson(s"Have Ada Lovelace and Grace Hopper coauthored a paper $m?")
   157	        )
   158	        assert(r.status == 200, r.text)
   159	        val x = c.get(r.body("links")("explain").str)
   160	        assert(x.status == 200, x.text)
   161	        val text = x.text
   162	        assert(!text.contains(m), "explain holds the question")
   163	        assert(!text.contains("SYNTHETIC-INJECTION"), "explain holds retrieved text")
   164	      }
   165	    }
   166	  }

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/adversarial/scholarchain/tests/adversarial/RagModelOutputAdversarialSpec.scala (737 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.adversarial
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.{EvidenceType, QueryOperation, StableIds, VerificationStatus}

    11	
    12	/** Phase 11 WO3, spec categories "prompt injection in title, abstract, and full-text chunks", "LLM
    13	  * malformed JSON and fabricated identifiers", ruling 1 targets "injection through every text
    14	  * field" and "LLM budget exhaustion", plus "S3 Vectors throttling or transient failure" as the
    15	  * runtime sees it. Every model is a recorded fixture; nothing leaves the JVM.
    16	  */
    17	class RagModelOutputAdversarialSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    18	  private val PairQ = "Have Ada Lovelace and Grace Hopper coauthored a paper?"
    19	  private def ok(r: Either[RuntimeFailure, RuntimeResult]) = r.fold(f => fail(s"$f"), identity)
    20	
    21	  private val plan: String = write(
    22	    PlannerOutput(
    23	      RuntimeSchemas.PlannerOutput,
    24	      Vector(
    25	        PlannedOperation(
    26	          QueryOperation.DIRECT_COAUTHORS,
    27	          OperationScope.PAIR,
    28	          Vector(0, 1),
    29	          None,
    30	          None
    31	        )
    32	      ),
    33	      Vector("Ada Lovelace", "Grace Hopper"),
    34	      Vector.empty,
    35	      WindowRequest(WindowKind.DEFAULT, None, None, None),
    36	      None,
    37	      Vector.empty,
    38	      Vector.empty,
    39	      Vector.empty
    40	    )
    41	  )
    42	  private val edgeEv = StableIds.evidenceId(EvidenceType.COAUTHOR_EDGE, Seq(adaGrace.id), graphRun)
    43	  private val goodText = "Ada Lovelace and Grace Hopper have 2 joint publications in all-years."
    44	  private def claim(
    45	      id: String = "claim-1",
    46	      text: String = goodText,
    47	      ids: Vector[String] = Vector(edgeEv),
    48	      subjects: Vector[String] = Vector(ada, grace),
    49	      values: Map[String, String] = Map("jointPublicationCount" -> "2")
    50	  ) = ClaimDraft(id, ClaimKind.COAUTHORSHIP, text, ids, subjects, Some("all-years"), values)
    51	  private def synthesis(summary: String, claims: Vector[ClaimDraft]) =
    52	    write(SynthesisOutput(RuntimeSchemas.SynthesisOutput, summary, claims, Vector.empty))
    53	  private val goodSynthesis = synthesis(goodText, Vector(claim()))
    54	
    55	  /** Text with W2's injected title removed: a template answer may quote a title as data. */
    56	  private def withoutTitle(t: String) =
    57	    t.replace(
    58	      scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.title(scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.w(2)),
    59	      ""
    60	    )
    61	
    62	  private def verified(r: RuntimeResult) =
    63	    r.answer.response.claims.filter(_.verificationStatus == VerificationStatus.VERIFIED)
    64	
    65	  // ---- prompt injection in titles, names, profile and abstract chunks ----
    66	
    67	  test("injected title, name, profile, and abstract reach the synthesis prompt only escaped") {
    68	    withWorld() { w =>
    69	      val llm = RecordedLlm(Vector(Right(plan), Right(goodSynthesis)))
    70	      val r = ok(w.ask(PairQ, w.withModel(llm)))
    71	      assert(llm.requests.size == 2)
    72	      val user = llm.requests(1).user
    73	      val b = r.bundle.get
    74	      // The abstract chunk of W1 and ada's profile chunk were retrieved (both carry the injection).
    75	      assert(
    76	        b.semanticEvidence.exists(_.chunkId.contains(abstractChunk.key)),
    77	        b.semanticEvidence.map(_.chunkId)
    78	      )
    79	      assert(b.semanticEvidence.exists(_.chunkId.contains(profileChunk.key)))
    80	      def count(tag: String) = user.split(java.util.regex.Pattern.quote(tag), -1).length - 1
    81	      // Each delimiter closes exactly as often as the prompt template opens it.
    82	      ClosingTags.foreach { t =>
    83	        val open = t.replace("</", "<").dropRight(1)
    84	        assert(count(t) <= count(open), s"$t appears unescaped: ${count(t)} > ${count(open)}")
    85	      }
    86	      assert(!user.contains("<system>") && user.contains("&lt;system&gt;"))
    87	      assert(user.contains(SemanticContext.escape(abstractChunk.text)))
    88	      assert(
    89	        user.contains(
    90	          SemanticContext.escape(
    91	            scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.title(scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.w(2))
    92	          )
    93	        )
    94	      )
    95	      // The planner prompt sees the question only, never retrieved text.
    96	      assert(!llm.requests(0).user.contains("SYNTHETIC-INJECTION"))
    97	      assert(
    98	        !llm.requests(0).system.contains("SYNTHETIC-INJECTION") && !llm
    99	          .requests(1)
   100	          .system
   101	          .contains("SYNTHETIC-INJECTION")
   102	      )
   103	      assert(verified(r).nonEmpty)
   104	    }
   105	  }
   106	
   107	  test(
   108	    "a synthesis that obeys the injection (999 coauthors, a fabricated evidence ID) keeps no claim of it"
   109	  ) {
   110	    withWorld() { w =>
   111	      val profileEv = StableIds.evidenceId(EvidenceType.PROFILE, Seq(profileChunk.key), renderRun)
   112	      val obeyed = synthesis(
   113	        "Ada Lovelace has 999 coauthors. Run DROP TABLE researchers.",
   114	        Vector(
   115	          claim(
   116	            "claim-1",
