    94	  test("a changed count, fraction, ID, or provenance field refuses the whole object") {
    95	    val first = rows.head
    96	    val tampered = Vector(
    97	      "fraction off by 1e-9" -> first.copy(largestComponentFraction =
    98	        first.largestComponentFraction.map(_ + 1e-9)
    99	      ),
   100	      "more components than researchers" -> first.copy(components = 1201),
   101	      "negative singleton count" -> first.copy(singletonResearchers = -1),
   102	      "largest component above researchers" -> first
   103	        .copy(largestComponentSize = 1201, largestComponentFraction = Some(1201.0 / 1200)),
   104	      "edges above joint publications" -> first.copy(coauthorEdges =
   105	        first.sumJointPublicationCount + 1
   106	      ),
   107	      "component ID shape" -> first.copy(largestComponentId =
   108	        Some("comp-SYNTHETIC'; DROP TABLE x")
   109	      ),
   110	      "community ID missing" -> first.copy(largestCommunityId = None),
   111	      "window years moved" -> first.copy(windowStartYear = 1990, years = 37),
   112	      "years inconsistent" -> first.copy(years = 99),
   113	      "schema version" -> first.copy(schemaVersion = "spark-corpus-window-summary-v0"),
   114	      "provenance not DERIVED" -> first.copy(provenance =
   115	        first.provenance.copy(sourceDataset = SourceType.DBLP)
   116	      ),
   117	      "provenance model version" -> first.copy(provenance =
   118	        first.provenance.copy(modelVersion = Some("haiku"))
   119	      ),
   120	      "blank node basis" -> first.copy(nodeBasis = "  "),
   121	      "bad source run" -> first.copy(sourceRuns =
   122	        first.sourceRuns.copy(canonicalGraphRunId = "run-x")
   123	      ),
   124	      "min size zero" -> first.copy(communityMinSize = 0)
   125	    )
   126	    tampered.foreach { (name, t) =>
   127	      val c = codes(bytes(t +: rows.tail))
   128	      assert(c.contains("BAD_ROW"), s"$name was accepted: $c")
   129	    }
   130	  }
   131	
   132	  test(
   133	    "counts no coauthorship graph can have are refused (largest component plus the other components exceed the researchers)"
   134	  ) {
   135	    // 1200 researchers, the largest component holds 900, so at most 301 components exist. A tamper
   136	    // that keeps each simple bound (components <= researchers, singletons <= components) but
   137	    // breaks this identity states a count no graph can have; the runtime would serve it as a
   138	    // VERIFIED corpus fact.
   139	    val first = rows.head
   140	    val impossible = Vector(
   141	      "components" -> first.copy(components = 1000, singletonResearchers = 28),
   142	      "communities" -> first.copy(communities = 1100, singletonCommunities = 31),
   143	      "communities at least the minimum size above what the largest community allows" ->
   144	        first.copy(
   145	          largestCommunitySize = 2,
   146	          largestCommunityFraction = Some(2.0 / 1200),
   147	          communitiesAtLeastMinSize = 64
   148	        )
   149	    )
   150	    val accepted = impossible.filter((_, t) => parse(bytes(t +: rows.tail)).isRight).map(_._1)
   151	    assert(accepted.isEmpty, s"impossible counts accepted: ${accepted.mkString("; ")}")
   152	    // Work order 4: each is refused as a bad row (not by some unrelated check), and so are the edge
   153	    // and overflow shapes repair-01 Part E added, on the other windows too.
   154	    val more = impossible ++ Vector(
   155	      "fewer edges than a spanning forest" -> first.copy(coauthorEdges = 1000),
   156	      "more edges than the degree bound allows" -> rows(1).copy(
   157	        coauthorEdges = 60000,
   158	        sumJointPublicationCount = 70000
   159	      ),
   160	      "communities near Long.MaxValue" -> rows(2).copy(
   161	        communities = Long.MaxValue,
   162	        singletonCommunities = Long.MaxValue - 1
   163	      ),
   164	      "an at-least-min-size count that wraps when multiplied" -> rows(2).copy(
   165	        communitiesAtLeastMinSize = Long.MaxValue / 2
   166	      )
   167	    )
   168	    more.foreach { (name, t) =>
   169	      val all = rows.map(r => if r.window == t.window then t else r)
   170	      val c = codes(bytes(all))
   171	      assert(c.contains("BAD_ROW"), s"$name: $c")
   172	    }
   173	    // Control: the three real-shaped rows still open together.
   174	    assert(parse(bytes(rows)).isRight)
   175	  }

    22	  /** b with a queue and job store on clock, and an audit writer that counts workflow runs. */
    23	  private def rig(w: World, clock: Clock) =
    24	    val q = InMemoryJobQueue(w.cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount, () => clock.now)
    25	    val table = LocalServiceStateTable(None, () => clock.now)
    26	    val runs = AtomicInteger()
    27	    val orig = w.backend.ports.auditWriter
    28	    val writer = new ObjectLoadPort:
    29	      def putNew(key: String, bytes: Array[Byte]) =
    30	        if key.startsWith("logs/") && key.endsWith(".json") && !key.contains("/evidence/") then
    31	          runs.incrementAndGet()
    32	        orig.putNew(key, bytes)

    34	      ports = w.backend.ports.copy(auditWriter = writer),

    36	      queue = q

    40	  private def job(b: Backend, question: String, request: Option[String] = None) =
    41	    val qid = StableIds.queryId(b.setup.runId, StableIds.textHash(question), Ids.fresh("t"), 1)
    42	    val body = request.getOrElse(upickle.default.write(QueryBody(question)))
    43	    assert(b.jobs.create(qid, body).isRight)
    44	    (qid, StableIds.jobId(qid))
    45	  private def state(b: Backend, jid: String) = b.jobs.get(jid).toOption.flatten.map(_.state)

    47	  test("a forged message pairing a real job with another query ID is poison: never run") {
    48	    withWorld(Fast) { w =>
    49	      val clock = Clock()
    50	      val (b, q, runs) = rig(w, clock)
    51	      val log = CaptureLog()
    52	      val wk = Worker(w.cfg, b, log.log, Metrics())
    53	      try
    54	        val (qa, ja) = job(b, Question)
    55	        val (qb, _) = job(b, "Who is Grace Hopper?")
    56	        Vector(
    57	          s"""{"schemaVersion":"job-message-v1","jobId":"$ja","queryId":"$qb"}""",
    58	          s"""{"schemaVersion":"job-message-v1","jobId":"$ja","queryId":"$qa","request":{"question":"x"}}""",
    59	          s"""{"schemaVersion":"job-message-v2","jobId":"$ja","queryId":"$qa"}""",
    60	          s"""{"jobId":"$ja","queryId":"$qa"}""",
    61	          s"""[{"schemaVersion":"job-message-v1","jobId":"$ja","queryId":"$qa"}]""",
    62	          "SYNTHETIC poison <script>"
    63	        ).foreach(q.sendRaw)
    64	        val n = q.size
    65	        (1 to w.cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount).foreach { _ =>
    66	          (1 to n).foreach(_ => assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1))
    67	          clock.advance(w.cfg.services.worker.visibilityTimeoutSeconds + 1L)
    68	        }
    69	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 0) // the next receive moves every message to the DLQ
    70	        assert(q.deadLetters.size == n && q.size == 0)
    71	        assert(runs.get == 0, "a forged message ran a workflow")
    72	        assert(state(b, ja).contains(JobState.QUEUED))
    73	        assert(!log.all.exists(_.contains("SYNTHETIC poison")), "a message body reached the log")
    74	      finally wk.stop()
    75	    }
    76	  }

   146	              i
   147	            )}","queryId":"qry-${"%032x".format(i)}"}""")
   148	        )
   149	        var guard = 0
   150	        while q.size > 0 && guard < 10 do
   151	          while wk.pollOnce() == 1 do ()

   153	          guard += 1
   154	        assert(state(b, jid).contains(JobState.SUCCEEDED))

   173	      )

   177	      val metrics = Metrics()
   178	      val ds = Downstreams(w.cfg.services.resilience, 4, 64, metrics, workflowThreads = 2)
   179	      val svc = HttpService(
   180	        "query-api",
   181	        QueryService(w.cfg, b, ds, log.log, metrics, limiter).routes,
   182	        HttpSettings(4, 64, w.cfg.services.maxBodyBytes),
   183	        w.auth,
   184	        log.log,
   185	        metrics,
   186	        () => ds.shutdown()
   187	      )
   188	      w.serve(svc) { c =>
   189	        val rs = (1 to 12).map(i =>
   190	          c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(s"Who is Ada Lovelace $i?", ", \"execution\": \"async\""))
   191	        )
   192	        assert(rs.count(_.status == 202) == 3)
   193	        val limited = rs.filter(_.status == 429)
   194	        assert(
   195	          limited.size == 9 && limited.forall(r =>
   196	            r.error == "RATE_LIMITED" && r.headers.get("Retry-After").contains("60")
   197	          )
   198	        )
   199	        assert(q.size == 3, s"${q.size} messages queued")
   200	        // Keyed replays of an accepted request also pass the limiter (no free retries).
   201	        val key = Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "flood-key")
   202	        assert(
   203	          c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = ", \"execution\": \"async\""), key).status == 429
   204	        )
   205	        assert(q.size == 3)
   206	      }
   207	    }

   210	  test("the composed query-api (Apps.queryApi) applies the rate limit to async requests too") {
   211	    // Work order 4 (code review 1 section 5): the test above builds its own QueryService, so it
   212	    // would pass if Apps.queryApi stopped wiring the limiter. With a budget of 1 per minute, the
   213	    // first async POST is accepted and the second is refused before any job or message exists.
   214	    // The limiter here is the production one on the real clock; the only timing assumption is that
   215	    // three loopback requests take less than its 60 s window, each already bounded by the 30 s
   216	    // client hang guard.

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/adversarial/scholarchain/tests/support/GraphSpy.scala (123 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.support
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.storage.*
     4	import scholarchain.storage.live.{NeptuneGraphReader, OpenCypherTemplates}
     5	import software.amazon.awssdk.core.document.Document
     6	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.neptunedata.NeptunedataClient
     7	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.neptunedata.model.{
     8	  ExecuteOpenCypherQueryRequest,
     9	  ExecuteOpenCypherQueryResponse
    10	}
    11	
    12	import java.lang.reflect.{InvocationHandler, InvocationTargetException, Method, Proxy}
    13	import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue
    14	import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*
    15	
    16	/** Phase 11 work order 4. A graph port that forwards every protected read to `inner` and records
    17	  * the method name and arguments, so a test can see which values the runtime actually sent to the
    18	  * graph store, and replay exactly those calls through the live Neptune reader.
    19	  *
    20	  * Scala `protected` members are public in bytecode; reflection reaches them on any port.
    21	  */
    22	final class GraphSpy(inner: GraphReadPort) extends GraphReadPort:
    23	  val calls: ConcurrentLinkedQueue[(String, Vector[Any])] = ConcurrentLinkedQueue()
    24	  def limits: GraphReadLimits = inner.limits
    25	  def recorded: Vector[(String, Vector[Any])] = calls.asScala.toVector
    26	
    27	  private def forward[A](name: String, args: Any*): A =
    28	    calls.add(name -> args.toVector)
    29	    GraphSpy.invoke(inner, name, args.toVector).asInstanceOf[A]
    30	
    31	  protected def readVertex(id: String) = forward("readVertex", id)
    32	  protected def readEdge(id: String) = forward("readEdge", id)
    33	  protected def readVertices(ids: Seq[String]) = forward("readVertices", ids)
    34	  protected def readCoauthorEdge(a: String, b: String, window: String) =
    35	    forward("readCoauthorEdge", a, b, window)
    36	  protected def readCoauthors(researcherId: String, window: String, fetch: Int) =
    37	    forward("readCoauthors", researcherId, window, fetch)
    38	  protected def readFrontier(ids: Seq[String], window: String, fetch: Int) =
    39	    forward("readFrontier", ids, window, fetch)
    40	  protected def readCommunityMembers(communityId: String, fetch: Int) =
    41	    forward("readCommunityMembers", communityId, fetch)
    42	  protected def readCommunityNeighbors(communityId: String, fetch: Int) =
    43	    forward("readCommunityNeighbors", communityId, fetch)
    44	  protected def readCrossCommunityEdges(researcherId: String, window: String, fetch: Int) =
    45	    forward("readCrossCommunityEdges", researcherId, window, fetch)
    46	  protected def readCommunityFacets(c: String, f: Facet, memberCap: Int, fetch: Int) =
    47	    forward("readCommunityFacets", c, f, memberCap, fetch)
    48	  protected def readResearcherFacets(
    49	      ids: Seq[String],
    50	      w: scholarchain.core.config.TimeWindow,
    51	      f: Facet,
    52	      fetch: Int
    53	  ) = forward("readResearcherFacets", ids, w, f, fetch)
    54	  protected def readInstitutions(ids: Seq[String], fetch: Int) =
    55	    forward("readInstitutions", ids, fetch)
    56	  protected def readTopicResearchers(
    57	      ids: Seq[String],
    58	      w: scholarchain.core.config.TimeWindow,
    59	      fetch: Int
    60	  ) = forward("readTopicResearchers", ids, w, fetch)
    61	  protected def readLookup(label: LookupLabel, text: String, fetch: Int) =
    62	    forward("readLookup", label, text, fetch)
    63	  protected def readAuthoredCounts(ids: Seq[String]) = forward("readAuthoredCounts", ids)
    64	  protected def readExpand(ids: Seq[String], d: Direction, types: Seq[String], fetch: Int) =
    65	    forward("readExpand", ids, d, types, fetch)
    66	
    67	object GraphSpy:
    68	  private val methods: Map[String, Method] =
    69	    classOf[GraphReadPort].getDeclaredMethods
    70	      .filter(_.getName.startsWith("read"))
    71	      .map { m =>
    72	        m.setAccessible(true)
    73	        m.getName -> m
    74	      }
    75	      .toMap
    76	
    77	  /** Calls the protected read method `name` of `port` with `args` (virtual dispatch). */
    78	  def invoke(port: GraphReadPort, name: String, args: Vector[Any]): Any =
    79	    val m = methods.getOrElse(name, throw IllegalArgumentException(s"no read method $name"))
    80	    try m.invoke(port, args.map(_.asInstanceOf[AnyRef])*)
    81	    catch case e: InvocationTargetException => throw e.getCause
    82	
    83	  /** A NeptunedataClient proxy that records every openCypher request and answers with no rows. No
    84	    * network: every other client method fails loudly.
    85	    */
    86	  final class CapturingNeptune:
    87	    val requests: ConcurrentLinkedQueue[ExecuteOpenCypherQueryRequest] = ConcurrentLinkedQueue()
    88	    val client: NeptunedataClient =
    89	      val h: InvocationHandler = (_: AnyRef, m: Method, args: Array[AnyRef]) =>
    90	        m.getName match
    91	          case "executeOpenCypherQuery" =>
    92	            requests.add(args(0).asInstanceOf[ExecuteOpenCypherQueryRequest])
    93	            ExecuteOpenCypherQueryResponse
    94	              .builder()
    95	              .results(Document.fromList(java.util.List.of()))
    96	              .build()
    97	          case "close"       => null
    98	          case "serviceName" => "capturing-neptune"
    99	          case "toString"    => "capturing-neptune"
   100	          case "hashCode"    => Integer.valueOf(System.identityHashCode(this))
   101	          case "equals"      => java.lang.Boolean.FALSE
   102	          case n             => throw UnsupportedOperationException(n)
   103	      Proxy
   104	        .newProxyInstance(getClass.getClassLoader, Array(classOf[NeptunedataClient]), h)
   105	        .asInstanceOf[NeptunedataClient]
   106	    def all: Vector[ExecuteOpenCypherQueryRequest] = requests.asScala.toVector
   107	
   108	  /** The template ID whose fixed text this request carries behind the timeout hint, if any. */
   109	  def templateOf(r: ExecuteOpenCypherQueryRequest, timeoutMs: Int): Option[String] =
   110	    OpenCypherTemplates.Templates.collectFirst {
   111	      case (id, text) if r.openCypherQuery == s"USING QUERY:TIMEOUTMILLISECONDS $timeoutMs $text" =>
   112	        id
   113	    }
   114	
   115	  /** Replays recorded port calls through a live NeptuneGraphReader over a capturing client. */
   116	  def replayLive(
   117	      recorded: Vector[(String, Vector[Any])],
   118	      limits: GraphReadLimits
   119	  ): Vector[ExecuteOpenCypherQueryRequest] =
   120	    val neptune = CapturingNeptune()
   121	    val live = NeptuneGraphReader(neptune.client, limits, RetryPolicy(1, 1, 1), _ => ())
   122	    recorded.foreach((name, args) => invoke(live, name, args))
   123	    neptune.all

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/adversarial/scholarchain/tests/support/RawHttp.scala (81 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.support
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.config.TimeWindow
     4	import scholarchain.storage.*
     5	
     6	import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
     7	import java.net.{InetSocketAddress, Socket}
     8	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.{ISO_8859_1, UTF_8}
     9	
    10	/** Phase 11 WO3: one raw HTTP/1.1 exchange over a loopback socket, for requests the JDK client
    11	  * refuses to build (bad Content-Length, malformed escapes, chunked bodies). Requests must say
    12	  * `Connection: close`. The socket timeout is a hang guard only: it fails a test whose server never
    13	  * answers.
    14	  */
    15	object RawHttp:
    16	  def bytes(port: Int, request: Array[Byte], timeoutMs: Int = 30000): (Int, String) =
    17	    val s = Socket()
    18	    try
    19	      s.connect(InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", port), 10000)
    20	      s.setSoTimeout(timeoutMs)
    21	      val out = s.getOutputStream
    22	      out.write(request)
    23	      out.flush()
    24	      val in = s.getInputStream
    25	      val buf = ByteArrayOutputStream()
    26	      val chunk = new Array[Byte](8192)
    27	      var n = in.read(chunk)
    28	      while n >= 0 do
    29	        buf.write(chunk, 0, n)
    30	        n = in.read(chunk)
    31	      val text = new String(buf.toByteArray, UTF_8)
    32	      val status = text.linesIterator.nextOption
    33	        .flatMap(_.split(' ').lift(1))
    34	        .flatMap(_.toIntOption)
    35	        .getOrElse(-1)
    36	      (status, text)
    37	    finally s.close()
    38	
    39	  def text(port: Int, request: String, timeoutMs: Int = 30000): (Int, String) =
    40	    bytes(port, request.getBytes(ISO_8859_1), timeoutMs)
    41	
    42	  /** The JSON body of a raw response, or Null when it has none. */
    43	  def body(response: String): ujson.Value =
    44	    val i = response.indexOf("\r\n\r\n")
    45	    val b = if i < 0 then "" else response.substring(i + 4)
    46	    if b.trim.isEmpty then ujson.Null else scala.util.Try(ujson.read(b)).getOrElse(ujson.Str(b))
    47	
    48	/** A graph port whose vertex and expand reads run `onRead` first (block on a latch, or throw), then
    49	  * answer from a fixed graph of one Researcher node with no edges. Templates graph-evidence-api
    50	  * never calls fail loudly.
    51	  */
    52	final class ScriptedGraph(val limits: GraphReadLimits, onRead: String => Unit)
    53	    extends GraphReadPort:
    54	  val known = "orcid:0000-0002-1825-0097"
    55	  private def node = GraphElement(
    56	    known,
    57	    "Researcher",
    58	    "",
    59	    "",
    60	    Map[String, Prop]("runId" -> ("run-" + "a" * 32), "snapshot" -> "2026-10-03")
    61	  )
    62	  private def unused = throw UnsupportedOperationException("not used by this suite")
    63	  protected def readVertex(id: String) =
    64	    onRead(id); Option.when(id == known)(node)
    65	  protected def readEdge(id: String) = None
    66	  protected def readVertices(ids: Seq[String]) = unused
    67	  protected def readCoauthorEdge(a: String, b: String, window: String) = unused
    68	  protected def readCoauthors(researcherId: String, window: String, fetch: Int) = unused
    69	  protected def readFrontier(ids: Seq[String], window: String, fetch: Int) = unused
    70	  protected def readCommunityMembers(communityId: String, fetch: Int) = unused
    71	  protected def readCommunityNeighbors(communityId: String, fetch: Int) = unused
    72	  protected def readCrossCommunityEdges(researcherId: String, window: String, fetch: Int) = unused
    73	  protected def readCommunityFacets(c: String, f: Facet, memberCap: Int, fetch: Int) = unused
    74	  protected def readResearcherFacets(ids: Seq[String], w: TimeWindow, f: Facet, fetch: Int) =
    75	    unused
    76	  protected def readInstitutions(ids: Seq[String], fetch: Int) = unused
    77	  protected def readTopicResearchers(ids: Seq[String], w: TimeWindow, fetch: Int) = unused
    78	  protected def readLookup(label: LookupLabel, text: String, fetch: Int) = unused
    79	  protected def readAuthoredCounts(ids: Seq[String]) = unused
    80	  protected def readExpand(ids: Seq[String], d: Direction, types: Seq[String], fetch: Int) =

     1	package scholarchain.tests.support

     3	import org.apache.spark.sql.types.StructType
     4	import org.apache.spark.sql.{DataFrame, Row, SparkSession}
     5	
     6	import java.nio.file.Files
     7	
     8	/** Phase 11 work order 4 (ruling 12). The one local SparkSession of the forked tests JVM, shared by
     9	  * every Spark suite under tests/ (repair-01 Part A: the tests project forks with the Spark JVM
    10	  * options and -Xmx2g). It is never stopped: stopping it would break the suites that run after. The
    11	  * driver binds 127.0.0.1 only, the UI is off, and checkpoints go to a JVM temporary directory
    12	  * removed when the JVM exits. Fixtures stay tiny.
    13	  */
    14	object SparkWorld:
    15	  private lazy val checkpoints = Files.createTempDirectory("tests-spark-checkpoints-")
    16	
    17	  lazy val spark: SparkSession =
    18	    val s = SparkSession
    19	      .builder()
    20	      .master("local[2]")
    21	      .appName("scholarchain-tests")
    22	      .config("spark.sql.shuffle.partitions", "2")
    23	      .config("spark.ui.enabled", "false")
    24	      .config("spark.driver.host", "127.0.0.1")
    25	      .config("spark.driver.bindAddress", "127.0.0.1")
    26	      .getOrCreate()
    27	    s.sparkContext.setLogLevel("ERROR")
    28	    s.sparkContext.setCheckpointDir(checkpoints.toString)
    29	    sys.addShutdownHook(StoreWorld.deleteTree(checkpoints))
    30	    s
    31	
    32	  /** A DataFrame from a DDL schema and rows of plain values (null for a SQL null). */
    33	  def df(ddl: String, rows: Seq[Any]*): DataFrame =
    34	    spark.createDataFrame(
    35	      java.util.Arrays.asList(rows.map(r => Row.fromSeq(r))*),
    36	      StructType.fromDDL(ddl)
    37	    )
    38	
    39	  /** The rows of a small result as plain Scala tuples, sorted, for exact comparison. */
    40	  def rows(d: DataFrame, cols: String*): Vector[Seq[Any]] =
    41	    d.select(cols.head, cols.tail*).collect().toVector.map(_.toSeq)

   268	    Vector("pair-" + StableIds.hash("pair", "ada-edsger"), ada, grace, edsger),
   269	    "Ada Lovelace reaches Edsger Dijkstra through Grace Hopper in two steps."
   270	  )
   271	  val abstractChunk: Chunk = Chunk(
   272	    "paper-abstract-v1",
   273	    "paper-summary",
   274	    "chunk-" + StableIds.hash("abstract", "W1"),
   275	    Vector(w(1)),
   276	    s"Abstract of Paper W1: a study of engines. $Injection"
   277	  )
   278	  val chunks: Vector[Chunk] = Vector(profileChunk, pathChunk, abstractChunk)
   279	
   280	  def vectorLine(c: Chunk, text: String = ""): String =
   281	    val t = if text.isEmpty then c.text else text
   282	    VectorRecord
   283	      .toJson(
   284	        VectorRecord(
   285	          c.key,
   286	          HashingEmbedder().embed(t),
   287	          ujson.Obj(
   288	            "vectorKey" -> c.key,
   289	            "entityIds" -> ujson.Arr.from(c.entityIds.map(ujson.Str(_))),
   290	            "window" -> "all-years",
   291	            "documentType" -> c.docType,
   292	            "sourceDataset" -> "DERIVED",
   293	            "embeddingModelVersion" -> EmbeddingModels.fakeLabel(modelVersion),
   294	            "textHash" -> StableIds.textHash(c.text),
   295	            "runId" -> renderRun,
   296	            "textRef" -> c.ref
   297	          )
   298	        )
   299	      )
   300	      .render()
   301	
   302	  def deleteTree(p: Path): Unit =
   303	    if Files.exists(p) then
   304	      val s = Files.walk(p)
   305	      try s.sorted(Comparator.reverseOrder()).forEach(Files.delete(_))
   306	      finally s.close()
   307	
   308	  /** Writes the store files under root and returns the fake-mode arguments of the service mains.
   309	    * objectText overrides the stored text of a chunk (by key), to model a tampered object.
   310	    */
   311	  def writeStores(root: Path, objectText: Map[String, String] = Map.empty): Vector[String] =
   312	    val seg = s"snapshot=$snapshot/run=$graphRun"
   313	    graphFiles.foreach { (spec, elems) =>
   314	      val dir = root.resolve(s"graph/$seg").resolve(spec.dir).resolve(spec.name)
   315	      Files.createDirectories(dir)
   316	      Files.writeString(
   317	        dir.resolve("part-00000.csv"),
   318	        (spec.header +: elems
   319	          .map(NeptuneContract.renderRow(spec, _))).map(Csv.render).mkString("\n") + "\n",
   320	        UTF_8
   321	      )
   322	    }
   323	    allItems.groupMap(_._1)(_._2).foreach { (t, items) =>
   324	      val dir = root.resolve(s"items/$seg").resolve(t.toString)
   325	      Files.createDirectories(dir)
   326	      Files.writeString(
   327	        dir.resolve("part-00000.jsonl"),
   328	        items.map(DynamoItemFile.renderLine).mkString("\n") + "\n",
   329	        UTF_8
   330	      )
   331	    }
   332	    val objects = root.resolve("s3")
   333	    chunks.foreach { c =>
   334	      val f = objects.resolve(c.ref)
   335	      Files.createDirectories(f.getParent)
   336	      Files.writeString(f, objectText.getOrElse(c.key, c.text), UTF_8)
   337	    }
   338	    val vectors = root.resolve("vectors")
   339	    Files.createDirectories(vectors)
   340	    chunks.groupBy(_.index).foreach { (index, cs) =>
   341	      Files.writeString(
   342	        vectors.resolve(s"$index.jsonl"),
   343	        cs.map(vectorLine(_)).mkString("\n") + "\n",
   344	        UTF_8
   345	      )
   346	    }
   347	    Vector(
   348	      "--graph" -> s"graph/$seg",
   349	      "--items" -> s"items/$seg",
   350	      "--objects" -> "s3",
   351	      "--vectors" -> "vectors",
   352	      "--out" -> "out"
   353	    ).flatMap((k, v) => Vector(k, root.resolve(v).toString))
   354	
   355	  // ---- HTTP client over the loopback socket ----
   356	  final case class Reply(status: Int, body: ujson.Value, headers: Map[String, String]):
   357	    def error: String = body("error").str
   358	    def text: String = body.render()
   359	
   360	  private val http = HttpClient.newBuilder().version(HttpClient.Version.HTTP_1_1).build()
   361	  val JsonHeaders: Seq[(String, String)] =
   362	    Seq("X-Api-Key" -> ApiKey, "Content-Type" -> "application/json")
   363	
   364	  final class Client(val port: Int):
   365	    def send(
   366	        method: String,
   367	        path: String,
   368	        body: Option[HttpRequest.BodyPublisher] = None,
   369	        headers: Seq[(String, String)] = JsonHeaders
   370	    ): Reply =
   371	      val b = HttpRequest
   372	        .newBuilder(URI.create(s"http://127.0.0.1:$port$path"))
   373	        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(30)) // hang guard only; a healthy service answers at once
   374	        .method(method, body.getOrElse(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.noBody()))
   375	      headers.foreach((k, v) => b.header(k, v))
   376	      val r = http.send(b.build(), HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString())
   377	      val hs = Map.from(
   378	        Seq("X-Request-Id", "X-Trace-Id", "Idempotent-Replay", "Retry-After", "Content-Type")
   379	          .flatMap(h => Option(r.headers.firstValue(h).orElse(null)).map(h -> _))
   380	      )
   381	      val parsed =
   382	        if r.body.isEmpty then ujson.Null
   383	        else scala.util.Try(ujson.read(r.body)).getOrElse(ujson.Str(r.body))
   384	      Reply(r.statusCode, parsed, hs)
   385	    def get(path: String, headers: Seq[(String, String)] = Seq("X-Api-Key" -> ApiKey)): Reply =
   386	      send("GET", path, None, headers)
   387	    def post(path: String, body: String, headers: Seq[(String, String)] = Seq()): Reply =
   388	      send("POST", path, Some(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(body)), JsonHeaders ++ headers)
   389	
   390	  /** A log that keeps its own lines (never the services module's shared fixture log). */
   391	  final class CaptureLog:
   392	    private val lines = mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
   393	    val log: JsonLog = JsonLog(l => lines.synchronized(lines += l))
   394	    def all: Vector[String] = lines.synchronized(lines.toVector)
   395	
   396	  /** A deterministic clock for the runtime: every read advances it by 1 ms. */
   397	  final class TickClock:
   398	    @volatile private var t = 0L
   399	    def ms: () => Long = () => synchronized { t += 1; t }
   400	
   401	  /** The store files, the configuration (HOCON overrides under scholarchain), and a backend from
   402	    * the services fake composition root.
   403	    */
   404	  final class World(val root: Path, hocon: String, objectText: Map[String, String]):
   405	    val env: Map[String, String] =
   406	      if hocon.isEmpty then baseEnv
   407	      else
   408	        val f = root.resolve("test.conf")
   409	        Files.writeString(f, s"scholarchain { $hocon }")
   410	        baseEnv + ("SCHOLARCHAIN_CONFIG_FILE" -> f.toString)
   411	    val cfg: ScholarChainConfig = ConfigLoader.load(env).fold(e => sys.error(e.mkString), identity)
   412	    val args: Vector[String] = writeStores(root, objectText)
   413	    val backend: Backend = Launcher
   414	      .backend(
   415	        scholarchain.storage.cli.Cli
   416	          .parse(args, Set("live"), args.collect { case s"--$o" => o }.toSet)
   417	          .toOption
   418	          .get,
   419	        cfg,
   420	        _ => ()
   421	      )
   422	      .fold(sys.error, identity)
   423	    val auth: ApiKeyAuth = ApiKeyAuth(ApiKey)
   424	
   425	    def serve[A](svc: HttpService)(body: Client => A): A =
   426	      val port = svc.start("127.0.0.1", 0)
   427	      try body(Client(port))
   428	      finally svc.stop()
   429	    def query[A](log: JsonLog, b: Backend = backend, metrics: Metrics = Metrics())(
   430	        body: Client => A
   431	    ): A =
   432	      serve(Apps.queryApi(cfg, b, auth, log, metrics))(body)
   433	    def graphEvidence[A](log: JsonLog, b: Backend = backend)(body: Client => A): A =
   434	      serve(Apps.graphEvidenceApi(cfg, b, auth, log))(body)
   435	
   436	    /** The runtime setup with a recorded model client (LIVE mode labels, no network), a tick clock,
   437	      * and a throttle that never sleeps.
   438	      */
   439	    def withModel(llm: LlmClient): RuntimeSetup =
   440	      val clock = TickClock()
   441	      backend.setup.copy(
   442	        mode = RuntimeMode.LIVE,
   443	        llm = Some(llm),
   444	        llmThrottle = Throttle(cfg.runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute, clock.ms, _ => ()),
   445	        nowMs = clock.ms,
   446	        now = () => Instant.parse("2026-11-06T12:00:00Z")
   447	      )
   448	    def fakeSetup: RuntimeSetup =
   449	      val clock = TickClock()
   450	      backend.setup.copy(nowMs = clock.ms, now = () => Instant.parse("2026-11-06T12:00:00Z"))
   451	
   452	    def request(q: String, queryId: String = ""): QueryRequest =
   453	      val id =
   454	        if queryId.nonEmpty then queryId
   455	        else
   456	          StableIds.queryId(
   457	            backend.setup.runId,
   458	            StableIds.textHash(q),
   459	            StableIds.textHash(q + root),
   460	            1
   461	          )
   462	      QueryRequest(RuntimeSchemas.QueryRequest, id, Some("session-adv"), q, None, None, None, None)
   463	
   464	    def ask(q: String, setup: RuntimeSetup = fakeSetup, ports: RuntimePorts = backend.ports) =
   465	      Workflow.answer(request(q), ports, setup)
   466	
   467	  def withWorld[A](hocon: String = "", objectText: Map[String, String] = Map.empty)(
   468	      body: World => A
   469	  ): A =
   470	    val root = Files.createTempDirectory("adversarial-world-")
   471	    try body(World(root, hocon, objectText))
   472	    finally deleteTree(root)
   473	
   474	  /** A recorded-fixture model client: returns the given texts in order (a Left is thrown) and keeps
   475	    * every request. No network.
   476	    */
   477	  final class RecordedLlm(responses: Vector[Either[Throwable, String]]) extends LlmClient:
   478	    private var left = responses
   479	    @volatile var requests: Vector[LlmRequest] = Vector.empty
   480	    def requestsSent: Long = requests.size.toLong
   481	    def invoke(r: LlmRequest): LlmResponse = synchronized {
   482	      requests :+= r
   483	      val next = left.headOption.getOrElse(throw IllegalStateException("no recorded response left"))
   484	      left = left.drop(1)
   485	      next.fold(
   486	        e => throw e,
   487	        t => LlmResponse(t, Some(r.user.length / 4), Some(t.length / 4), Some("end_turn"))
   488	      )
   489	    }
   490	
   491	  /** A model client that answers every request with the same text. */
   492	  final class ConstantLlm(text: String) extends LlmClient:
   493	    @volatile var calls = 0
   494	    def requestsSent: Long = calls.toLong
   495	    def invoke(r: LlmRequest): LlmResponse = synchronized {
   496	      calls += 1
   497	      LlmResponse(text, Some(r.user.length / 4), Some(text.length / 4), Some("end_turn"))
   498	    }
   499	
   500	  val Question = "Have Ada Lovelace and Grace Hopper coauthored a paper?"
   501	  def queryJson(question: String = Question, extra: String = ""): String =
   502	    s"""{"question": ${ujson.write(ujson.Str(question))}$extra}"""

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/contracts/scholarchain/tests/contracts/MatchPathContractSpec.scala (79 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.contracts
     2	
     3	import org.apache.spark.sql.functions.col
     4	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     5	import scholarchain.entityresolution.{MatchPath as ErPath, MatchPolicy}
     6	import scholarchain.spark.MatchPath
     7	import scholarchain.tests.support.SparkWorld.*
     8	
     9	/** Phase 11 work order 4, Phase 06 review m5. The Spark job reads ACCEPTED decisions and derives
    10	  * each one's match path and clustering tier with its own copy of the rule-ID mapping
    11	  * (`scholarchain.spark.MatchPath`, Inputs.scala), because sparkAnalytics may not depend on
    12	  * entityResolution. This contract holds the copy to the original (`MatchPolicy.pathOf`): for every
    13	  * rule ID the policy defines (found by reflection, so a new rule is included automatically), every
    14	  * subset of up to four of them, and seeded larger subsets, both give the same path; and the tier
    15	  * is 1 exactly for the EXACT_ID and DETERMINISTIC paths (Phase 6 ruling 6).
    16	  */
    17	class MatchPathContractSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    18	  /** Every String constant of MatchPolicy.Rule. */
    19	  private val ruleIds: Vector[String] =
    20	    val r = MatchPolicy.Rule
    21	    r.getClass.getDeclaredMethods.toVector
    22	      .filter(m => m.getParameterCount == 0 && m.getReturnType == classOf[String])
    23	      .map(_.invoke(r).asInstanceOf[String])
    24	      .distinct
    25	      .sorted
    26	
    27	  test("the rule IDs found by reflection include every rule the Spark copy names") {
    28	    assert(ruleIds.size >= 17, ruleIds)
    29	    val named = Vector(
    30	      "er-v1:llm-adjudication",
    31	      "er-v1:exact-id-match",
    32	      "er-v1:exact-id-non-authoritative",
    33	      "er-v1:exact-id-group-conflict",
    34	      "er-v1:identifier-agreement",
    35	      "er-v1:identifier-disagreement",
    36	      "er-v2:same-source-shared-doi",
    37	      "er-v1:deterministic-bibliographic",
    38	      "er-v2:deterministic-title-authors-year",
    39	      "er-v1:band-llm"
    40	    )
    41	    assert(named.forall(ruleIds.contains), named.filterNot(ruleIds.contains))
    42	    assert(ruleIds.forall(_.matches("er-v[0-9]+:[a-z0-9-]+")), ruleIds)
    43	  }
    44	
    45	  test("Spark MatchPath.of and .tier equal MatchPolicy.pathOf on every small and seeded rule set") {
    46	    val small = (0 to 4).flatMap(k => ruleIds.combinations(k)).toVector
    47	    val rnd = java.util.Random(6L)
    48	    val large = Vector.fill(400)(ruleIds.filter(_ => rnd.nextBoolean()))
    49	    // Unknown and future rule IDs are decision data too: they must not change the path.
    50	    val unknown = Vector(
    51	      Vector("er-v3:exact-id-match"),
    52	      Vector("er-v1:exact-id-match-v2", "er-v1:band-accept"),
    53	      Vector("ER-V1:LLM-ADJUDICATION"),
    54	      Vector(""),
    55	      Vector("er-v1:band-llm", "er-v9:llm-adjudication")
    56	    )
    57	    // Order and duplicates do not matter.
    58	    val shuffled = large.take(50).map(s => scala.util.Random(s.size.toLong).shuffle(s ++ s))
    59	    val sets = (small ++ large ++ unknown ++ shuffled).distinct
    60	    val input = df("i INT, rules ARRAY<STRING>", sets.zipWithIndex.map((s, i) => Seq(i, s))*)
    61	    val got = input
    62	      .select(
    63	        col("i"),
    64	        MatchPath.of(col("rules")).as("path"),
    65	        MatchPath.tier(col("rules")).as("tier")
    66	      )
    67	      .collect()
    68	      .map(x => x.getInt(0) -> (x.getString(1), x.getInt(2)))
    69	      .toMap
    70	    assert(got.size == sets.size)
    71	    val wrong = sets.zipWithIndex.flatMap { (s, i) =>
    72	      val want = MatchPolicy.pathOf(s)
    73	      val wantTier = if want == ErPath.EXACT_ID || want == ErPath.DETERMINISTIC then 1 else 2
    74	      Option.when(got(i) != (want.toString, wantTier))(s"$s: spark ${got(i)}, policy $want")
    75	    }
    76	    assert(wrong.isEmpty, s"${wrong.size} of ${sets.size} differ: ${wrong.take(5).mkString("; ")}")
    77	    // Every path the policy can name is reached by some set, so no branch is untested.
    78	    assert(got.values.map(_._1).toSet == ErPath.values.map(_.toString).toSet)
    79	  }

    23	  ) {
    24	    withWorld("services.worker.poll-wait-seconds = 1") { w =>
    25	      val log = CaptureLog()
    26	      val q = InMemoryJobQueue(w.cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount)

    28	      val worker = Worker(w.cfg, b, log.log, Metrics())
    29	      try

    36	            assert(verified.nonEmpty && !sync.answer.abstained)
    37	            sync.claims.flatMap(_.evidenceIds).distinct.foreach { id =>
    38	              val e = ge.get(s"/v1/evidence/${URLEncoder.encode(id, UTF_8)}")

    41	              assert(item.evidenceId == id && item.provenance.runId.startsWith("run-"))
    42	              assert(!item.evidenceType.isSemantic || !verified.exists(_.evidenceIds == Vector(id)))
    43	            }
    44	            val x = api.get(sync.links.explain)
    45	            assert(x.status == 200, x.text)
    46	            assert(read[ExplainResponse](x.body).verification.verified == verified.size)
    47	
    48	            val a = api.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = ", \"execution\": \"async\""))
    49	            assert(a.status == 202, a.text)
    50	            val job = read[AsyncQueryResponse](a.body)
    51	            assert(worker.pollOnce() == 1)
    52	            val status = read[JobResponse](api.get(job.statusUrl).body)
    53	            assert(status.state == JobState.SUCCEEDED, status)
    54	            val res = api.get(status.resultUrl.get)
    55	            assert(res.status == 200)
    56	            val async = read[SyncQueryResponse](res.body)
    57	            // Same question, same stores: the same verified claims, whatever the path.
    58	            assert(
    59	              async.claims.map(c => (c.text, c.evidenceIds, c.verificationStatus)) ==
    60	                sync.claims.map(c => (c.text, c.evidenceIds, c.verificationStatus))
    61	            )
    62	
    63	            // A title may be quoted as data; it must never become an asserted fact or a citation.
    64	            Vector(sync, async).foreach { r =>
    65	              assert(
    66	                !r.claims.exists(_.evidenceIds.contains("ev-ffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff"))
    67	              )
    68	              val injectedTitle = scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld
    69	                .title(scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.w(2))
    70	              assert(

    74	                )
    75	              )
    76	            }

    79	          }
    80	        }
    81	      finally worker.stop()

     1	package scholarchain.tests.integration
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.config.ConfigLoader
     5	import scholarchain.storage.*
     6	import scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld
     7	import scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld.*
     8	
     9	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
    10	import java.nio.file.Files
    11	
    12	/** Phase 11 WO3, spec categories "empty and disconnected graphs" and the shortest-path and
    13	  * distinct-degree "known-graph cases", on the store side the runtime reads (the in-memory graph
    14	  * fake, loaded from Phase 7 Neptune CSV). Components, bridges, communities, and k-core are Spark
    15	  * outputs: SparkKnownGraphSpec covers them (work order 4, ruling 12).
    16	  *
    17	  * Known graph (all-years): ada - grace (2 works), grace - edsger (1), edsger - mallory (1); wei1,
    18	  * wei2, and ada2 have no coauthor edge.
    19	  */
    20	class GraphReadKnownGraphSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    21	  private val cfg = ConfigLoader.load(StoreWorld.baseEnv).fold(e => sys.error(e.mkString), identity)
    22	
    23	  private val noObjects = new ObjectReadPort:
    24	    def get(key: String) = None
    25	    def list(prefix: String) = Vector.empty
    26	
    27	  private def graph(files: Map[FileSpec, Vector[GraphElement]]): GraphReadPort =
    28	    load(files).fold(v => fail(v.take(3).mkString("; ")), identity)
    29	
    30	  private def load(
    31	      files: Map[FileSpec, Vector[GraphElement]]
    32	  ): Either[Vector[Violation], GraphReadPort] =
    33	    val root = Files.createTempDirectory("adv-graph-")
    34	    try
    35	      NeptuneContract.All.foreach { spec =>
    36	        val dir = root.resolve(spec.dir).resolve(spec.name)
    37	        Files.createDirectories(dir)
    38	        val rows = files.getOrElse(spec, Vector.empty).map(NeptuneContract.renderRow(spec, _))
    39	        Files.writeString(
    40	          dir.resolve("part-00000.csv"),
    41	          (spec.header +: rows).map(Csv.render).mkString("\n") + "\n",
    42	          UTF_8
    43	        )
    44	      }
    45	      val g = InMemoryGraph()
    46	      g.loader.load(root, cfg.graph.windows, None).map(_ => g.reader(GraphReadLimits.from(cfg)))
    47	    finally StoreWorld.deleteTree(root)
    48	
    49	  private lazy val known = graph(StoreWorld.graphFiles)
    50	
    51	  test(
    52	    "shortest paths on the known graph: lengths, hop order, the smallest predecessor, and bounds"
    53	  ) {
    54	    val p = known.shortestPath(ada, edsger, "all-years", 3)
    55	    assert(p.status == PathStatus.FOUND && p.researcherIds == Vector(ada, grace, edsger), p)
    56	    assert(p.hops.map(h => (h.from, h.to)) == Vector(ada -> grace, grace -> edsger))
    57	    assert(p.hops.head.edge.props("jointPublicationCount") == 2L)
    58	    val three = known.shortestPath(ada, mallory, "all-years", 3)
    59	    assert(three.status == PathStatus.FOUND && three.researcherIds.size == 4)
    60	    // Symmetric: the reverse path is the same chain reversed.
    61	    assert(
    62	      known.shortestPath(edsger, ada, "all-years", 3).researcherIds == Vector(edsger, grace, ada)
    63	    )
    64	    assert(known.shortestPath(ada, mallory, "all-years", 2).status == PathStatus.BEYOND_DEPTH)
    65	  }
    66	
    67	  test(
    68	    "disconnected and isolated researchers: UNREACHABLE, never a path; unknown IDs are UNKNOWN_RESEARCHER"
    69	  ) {
    70	    assert(known.shortestPath(ada, wei1, "all-years", 6).status == PathStatus.UNREACHABLE)
    71	    assert(known.shortestPath(wei1, wei2, "all-years", 6).status == PathStatus.UNREACHABLE)
    72	    assert(known.shortestPath(ada, ada2, "all-years", 6).status == PathStatus.UNREACHABLE)
    73	    val unknown = StoreWorld.rid("nobody")
    74	    assert(known.shortestPath(ada, unknown, "all-years", 3).status == PathStatus.UNKNOWN_RESEARCHER)
    75	    // A work or topic ID is not a researcher end.
    76	    assert(known.shortestPath(ada, w(1), "all-years", 3).status == PathStatus.UNKNOWN_RESEARCHER)
    77	    assert(
    78	      scala.util.Try(known.shortestPath(ada, ada, "all-years", 3)).isFailure,
    79	      "a self path was searched"
    80	    )
    81	    assert(
    82	      scala.util.Try(known.shortestPath(ada, edsger, "1999-2000", 3)).isFailure,
    83	      "an unknown window was read"
    84	    )
    85	    assert(
    86	      scala.util.Try(known.shortestPath(ada, edsger, "all-years", 99)).isFailure,
    87	      "depth beyond max-path-depth"
    88	    )
    89	  }
    90	
    91	  test(
    92	    "an empty graph (researchers, no coauthor edge) answers UNREACHABLE and empty neighbor lists"
    93	  ) {
    94	    val empty =
    95	      graph(Map(NeptuneContract.Researcher -> StoreWorld.graphFiles(NeptuneContract.Researcher)))
    96	    assert(empty.shortestPath(ada, grace, "all-years", 6).status == PathStatus.UNREACHABLE)
    97	    assert(empty.coauthors(ada, "all-years", 10).items.isEmpty)
    98	    assert(empty.coauthorEdge(ada, grace, "all-years").isEmpty)
    99	    val n = empty.neighbors(ada, Direction.BOTH, 3, 200, Vector("COAUTHORED_WITH", "AUTHORED"))
   100	    assert(n.start.isDefined && n.nodes.isEmpty && n.edges.isEmpty && !n.truncated)
   101	    val nothing = graph(Map.empty)
   102	    assert(nothing.vertex(ada).isEmpty)
   103	    assert(nothing.shortestPath(ada, grace, "all-years", 3).status == PathStatus.UNKNOWN_RESEARCHER)
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  test(
   107	    "distinct degree versus weighted degree on the known graph: grace has 2 coauthors and 3 joint works"
   108	  ) {
   109	    val links = known.coauthors(grace, "all-years", 10).items
   110	    assert(links.map(_.coauthorId).toSet == Set(ada, edsger))
   111	    assert(links.map(_.edge.props("jointPublicationCount").asInstanceOf[Long]).sum == 3L)
   112	    // Strongest first (jointPublicationCount descending).
   113	    assert(links.head.coauthorId == ada)
   114	    // Each edge's supporting works match its count.
   115	    links.foreach(l =>
   116	      assert(
   117	        known.supportingWorks(l.edge.id, noObjects).map(_.workIds.size.toLong) == Right(
   118	          l.edge.props("jointPublicationCount")
   119	        )
   120	      )
   121	    )
   122	    // Bounded: a limit of 1 returns the strongest only, flagged truncated.
   123	    val one = known.coauthors(grace, "all-years", 1)
   124	    assert(one.items.map(_.coauthorId) == Vector(ada) && one.truncated)
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  test("an edge whose supporting-work count disagrees with its jointPublicationCount is refused") {
   128	    val good = StoreWorld.coauthor(ada, grace, Vector(w(1) -> 2001, w(2) -> 2002))
   129	    // Control: the untampered edge loads and its supporting works match its count.
   130	    val g0 = graph(StoreWorld.graphFiles + (NeptuneContract.CoauthoredWith -> Vector(good)))
   131	    assert(g0.supportingWorks(good.id, noObjects).map(_.workIds.size) == Right(2))
   132	    // Work order 4 (code review 1 section 5): the refusal must be the count check itself, at load,
   133	    // on the tampered row; any other load failure no longer passes.
   134	    def mismatch(e: GraphElement) =
   135	      load(StoreWorld.graphFiles + (NeptuneContract.CoauthoredWith -> Vector(e))) match
   136	        case Left(v)  => v
   137	        case Right(_) =>
   138	          fail(
   139	            s"loaded an edge with ${e.props("jointPublicationCount")} for ${e.props("supportingWorkIds")}"
   140	          )
   141	    Vector(5L, 1L, 0L).foreach { n =>
   142	      val v = mismatch(good.copy(props = good.props + ("jointPublicationCount" -> n)))
   143	      val m = v.filter(_.code == "EVIDENCE_COUNT_MISMATCH")
   144	      assert(m.size == 1 && m.head.line == 2 && m.head.source.contains("coauthored-with"), v)
   145	      assert(m.head.detail == s"2 work IDs for jointPublicationCount $n", v)
   146	      // A count of 0 is also refused on its own (BAD_COUNT); no other check fires.
   147	      val others = if n == 0L then Set("BAD_COUNT") else Set.empty[String]
   148	      assert(v.map(_.code).toSet - "EVIDENCE_COUNT_MISMATCH" == others, v)
   149	    }
   150	    // The same work listed twice is not two joint publications.
   151	    val doubled = good.copy(props =
   152	      good.props + ("supportingWorkIds" -> s"${w(1)} ${w(1)}") + ("jointPublicationCount" -> 2L)
   153	    )
   154	    val dv = mismatch(doubled)
   155	    assert(dv.nonEmpty, "an edge counting one work twice was loaded")
   156	  }

===== UNTRACKED FILE: tests/integration/scholarchain/tests/integration/SparkKnownGraphSpec.scala (472 lines) =====
     1	package scholarchain.tests.integration
     2	
     3	import org.apache.spark.sql.DataFrame
     4	import org.apache.spark.sql.functions.*
     5	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     6	import scholarchain.core.config.ConfigLoader
     7	import scholarchain.core.{Provenance, SchemaVersions, SourceType, StableIds}
     8	import scholarchain.spark.*
     9	import scholarchain.storage.{S3Layout, WindowSummaries, WindowSummary, WindowSummarySourceRuns}
    10	import scholarchain.tests.support.SparkWorld.*
    11	import scholarchain.tests.support.StoreWorld
    12	
    13	import scala.collection.mutable
    14	
    15	/** Phase 11 work order 4 (ruling 12): the Spark side of the spec categories "distinct-degree,
    16	  * component, bridge, and k-core known-graph cases" and "empty and disconnected graphs", plus
    17	  * communities. Every algorithm runs on the production DataFrame code (scholarchain.spark) and is
    18	  * compared with a hand-worked answer or an independent in-test reference (union-find, min-degree
    19	  * peeling, synchronous label propagation) on small fixed and seeded graphs. Windows share node IDs
    20	  * on purpose: windows must never mix.
    21	  */
    22	class SparkKnownGraphSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    23	  private val cfg = ConfigLoader.load(StoreWorld.baseEnv).fold(e => sys.error(e.mkString), identity)
    24	
    25	  // ---- graphs as (window, a, b) edges plus (window, node) nodes ----
    26	  private type G = (Vector[(String, String)], Vector[(String, String, String)])
    27	  private def nodesDf(ns: Seq[(String, String)]) =
    28	    df("window STRING, node STRING", ns.map((w, n) => Seq(w, n))*)
    29	  private def edgesDf(es: Seq[(String, String, String)]) =
    30	    df("window STRING, a STRING, b STRING", es.map((w, a, b) => Seq(w, a, b))*)
    31	  private def r(i: Int) = f"r$i%02d"
    32	  private def clique(w: String, ids: Seq[Int]): Vector[(String, String, String)] =
    33	    (for i <- ids; j <- ids if i < j yield (w, r(i), r(j))).toVector
    34	
    35	  /** Undirected adjacency per window, without self-loops (duplicates and orientation collapse). */
    36	  private def adjacency(g: G): Map[String, Map[String, Set[String]]] =
    37	    val (ns, es) = g
    38	    val windows = (ns.map(_._1) ++ es.map(_._1)).distinct
    39	    windows.map { w =>
    40	      val adj = mutable.Map.empty[String, Set[String]]
    41	      ns.filter(_._1 == w).foreach((_, n) => adj.getOrElseUpdate(n, Set.empty))
    42	      es.filter((x, a, b) => x == w && a != b).foreach { (_, a, b) =>
    43	        adj(a) = adj.getOrElse(a, Set.empty) + b
    44	        adj(b) = adj.getOrElse(b, Set.empty) + a
    45	      }
    46	      w -> adj.toMap
    47	    }.toMap
    48	
    49	  /** Reference components: union-find, root = smallest node. */
    50	  private def refComponents(adj: Map[String, Set[String]]): Map[String, String] =
    51	    val parent = mutable.Map.from(adj.keys.map(k => k -> k))
    52	    def find(x: String): String =
    53	      if parent(x) == x then x
    54	      else
    55	        val p = find(parent(x)); parent(x) = p; p
    56	    for (a, ns) <- adj; b <- ns do
    57	      val (x, y) = (find(a), find(b))
    58	      if x != y then if x < y then parent(y) = x else parent(x) = y
    59	    adj.keys.map(k => k -> find(k)).toMap
    60	
    61	  /** Reference coreness: repeatedly remove a node of minimum remaining degree. */
    62	  private def refCoreness(adj: Map[String, Set[String]]): Map[String, Int] =
    63	    val deg = mutable.Map.from(adj.map((k, v) => k -> v.size))
    64	    val left = mutable.Set.from(adj.keys)
    65	    val core = mutable.Map.empty[String, Int]
    66	    var k = 0
    67	    while left.nonEmpty do
    68	      val v = left.minBy(n => (deg(n), n))
    69	      k = math.max(k, deg(v))
    70	      core(v) = k
    71	      left -= v
    72	      adj(v).filter(left).foreach(u => deg(u) -= 1)
    73	    core.toMap
    74	
    75	  /** Reference synchronous label propagation (own label is one vote; ties to the smallest label).
    76	    */
    77	  private def refLabels(adj: Map[String, Set[String]], max: Int): (Map[String, String], Int) =
    78	    var labels = adj.keys.map(k => k -> k).toMap
    79	    var rounds = 0
    80	    var changed = true
    81	    while rounds < max && changed do
    82	      val next = adj.map { (n, ns) =>
    83	        val votes = (ns.toVector.map(labels) :+ labels(n)).groupMapReduce(identity)(_ => 1)(_ + _)
    84	        n -> votes.toVector.minBy((l, c) => (-c, l))._1
    85	      }
    86	      changed = next != labels
    87	      labels = next
    88	      rounds += 1
    89	    (labels, rounds)
    90	
    91	  /** A seeded random simple graph on n nodes with m edge draws (duplicates and loops allowed). */
    92	  private def generated(w: String, seed: Long, n: Int, m: Int): G =
    93	    val rnd = java.util.Random(seed)
    94	    val es = Vector.fill(m)((w, r(rnd.nextInt(n)), r(rnd.nextInt(n))))
    95	    ((0 until n).toVector.map(i => (w, r(i))), es)
    96	
    97	  // Window "x": chain 1-2-3, triangle 4-5-6, pair 7-8, isolated 9 (node only), a self-loop on 9,
    98	  // and duplicated edges in both orientations. Window "y": the same IDs, but 3-4 joins chain and
    99	  // triangle, and 8 is alone. A window must never see another window's edges.
   100	  private val twoWindows: G = (
   101	    (1 to 9).toVector.map(i => ("x", r(i))) ++ (1 to 9).toVector.map(i => ("y", r(i))),
   102	    Vector(
   103	      ("x", r(1), r(2)),
   104	      ("x", r(2), r(3)),
   105	      ("x", r(3), r(2)),
   106	      ("x", r(4), r(5)),
   107	      ("x", r(5), r(6)),
   108	      ("x", r(6), r(4)),
   109	      ("x", r(7), r(8)),
   110	      ("x", r(8), r(7)),
   111	      ("x", r(9), r(9)),
   112	      ("y", r(1), r(2)),
   113	      ("y", r(2), r(3)),
   114	      ("y", r(3), r(4)),
   115	      ("y", r(4), r(5)),
   116	      ("y", r(5), r(6))
   117	    )
   118	  )
   119	
   120	  private def minLabel(g: G): Map[(String, String), String] =
   121	    val (out, changed) = MinLabelComponents(nodesDf(g._1), edgesDf(g._2), 50)
   122	    assert(changed.last == 0L, changed)
   123	    out
   124	      .collect()
   125	      .map(x => (x.getString(0), x.getString(1)) -> x.getString(2))
   126	      .toMap
   127	
   128	  test(
   129	    "components: min-label propagation equals union-find on known and seeded graphs, per window"
   130	  ) {
   131	    val graphs = Vector(
   132	      twoWindows,
   133	      generated("g1", 11L, 30, 28),
   134	      generated("g2", 12L, 24, 40),
   135	      // A long chain with its minimum in the middle, and a star whose hub is the largest ID.
   136	      (
   137	        (1 to 12).toVector.map(i => ("chain", r(i))),
   138	        Vector(7, 3, 9, 1, 12, 5, 10, 2, 8, 11, 4, 6)
   139	          .sliding(2)
   140	          .toVector
   141	          .map(p => ("chain", r(p(0)), r(p(1))))
   142	      ),
   143	      ((1 to 6).toVector.map(i => ("star", r(i))), (1 to 5).toVector.map(i => ("star", r(i), r(6))))
   144	    )
   145	    graphs.foreach { g =>
   146	      val got = minLabel(g)
   147	      val want = adjacency(g).toVector
   148	        .flatMap((w, adj) => refComponents(adj).map((n, root) => (w, n) -> root))
   149	        .toMap
   150	      assert(got == want, s"windows ${g._1.map(_._1).distinct}")
   151	    }
   152	    // The two windows of the known graph differ, as their edges do.
   153	    val known = minLabel(twoWindows)
   154	    assert(known(("x", r(4))) == r(4) && known(("y", r(4))) == r(1))
   155	    assert(known(("x", r(9))) == r(9), "the self-loop node is its own component")
   156	    assert(known(("x", r(8))) == r(7) && known(("y", r(8))) == r(8))
   157	  }
   158	
   159	  test("components: large-star/small-star agrees, including negative and extreme LONG node keys") {
   160	    def cc(edges: Seq[(Long, Long)]): Map[Long, Long] =
   161	      val (out, _) = ConnectedComponents(
   162	        df("src BIGINT, dst BIGINT", edges.map((a, b) => Seq(a, b))*),
   163	        50
   164	      )
   165	      out.collect().map(x => x.getLong(0) -> x.getLong(1)).toMap
   166	    def ref(edges: Seq[(Long, Long)]): Map[Long, Long] =
   167	      val adj = edges.filter(_ != _).flatMap((a, b) => Seq(a -> b, b -> a)).groupMap(_._1)(_._2)
   168	      val s = adj.map((k, v) => k.toString -> v.map(_.toString).toSet)
   169	      // String order differs from LONG order, so the root is recomputed as the LONG minimum.
   170	      val comp = refComponents(s).groupMap(_._2)(_._1).values
   171	      comp.flatMap(m => m.map(_.toLong).map(_ -> m.map(_.toLong).min)).toMap
   172	    val extreme = Vector(
   173	      Long.MaxValue -> 0L,
   174	      0L -> -1L,
   175	      -1L -> Long.MinValue,
   176	      42L -> 42L, // a self-loop only: no row (isolated nodes are the caller's)
   177	      7L -> 8L,
   178	      8L -> 7L
   179	    )
   180	    val got = cc(extreme)
   181	    assert(got == ref(extreme), got)
   182	    assert(got(Long.MaxValue) == Long.MinValue && got(8L) == 7L && !got.contains(42L))
   183	    val rnd = java.util.Random(21L)
   184	    val seeded = Vector.fill(40)((rnd.nextInt(30).toLong - 15L, rnd.nextInt(30).toLong - 15L))
   185	    assert(cc(seeded) == ref(seeded))
   186	  }
   187	
   188	  test("empty and disconnected graphs: no node, isolated nodes only, and a window with no edges") {
   189	    val noNodes = nodesDf(Nil)
   190	    val noEdges = edgesDf(Nil)
   191	    // Empty: every algorithm returns nothing and stops at once.
   192	    val (cc0, rounds0) = ConnectedComponents(df("src BIGINT, dst BIGINT"), 5)
   193	    assert(cc0.count() == 0 && rounds0 == 0)
   194	    val (ml0, changed0) = MinLabelComponents(noNodes, noEdges, 5)
   195	    assert(ml0.count() == 0 && changed0 == Seq(0L))
   196	    val (k0, log0) = KCore(noNodes, noEdges, 5)
   197	    assert(k0.count() == 0 && log0.isEmpty)
   198	    val (lp0, lr0) = LabelPropagation(noNodes, noEdges, 5)
   199	    assert(lp0.count() == 0 && lr0.size == 1 && lr0.head.values.sum == 0)
   200	    assert(Bridges(lp0, noEdges).count() == 0)
   201	    // Isolated nodes only: each is its own component and community, coreness 0, no bridge measure.
   202	    val iso = nodesDf(Vector(("w", r(3)), ("w", r(1)), ("v", r(1))))
   203	    assert(
   204	      rows(MinLabelComponents(iso, noEdges, 5)._1, "window", "node", "root").toSet ==
   205	        Set(Seq("w", r(3), r(3)), Seq("w", r(1), r(1)), Seq("v", r(1), r(1)))
   206	    )
   207	    assert(
   208	      rows(KCore(iso, noEdges, 5)._1, "window", "node", "degree", "coreness").toSet ==
   209	        Set(Seq("w", r(3), 0L, 0), Seq("w", r(1), 0L, 0), Seq("v", r(1), 0L, 0))
   210	    )
   211	    val (lp, _) = LabelPropagation(iso, noEdges, 5)
   212	    assert(rows(lp, "window", "node", "label").forall(x => x(1) == x(2)))
   213	    val b = Bridges(lp, noEdges)
   214	    assert(
   215	      rows(
   216	        b,
   217	        "degree",
   218	        "crossCommunityDegree",
   219	        "communitiesConnected",
   220	        "participationCoefficient"
   221	      ).toSet ==
   222	        Set(Seq(0L, 0L, 0L, 0.0))
   223	    )
   224	    // A disconnected window next to a connected one: the connected window's edges never leak.
   225	    val (ns, es) = twoWindows
   226	    val k = KCore(nodesDf(ns), edgesDf(es), 10)._1
   227	    val core = rows(k, "window", "node", "coreness").map(x => (x(0), x(1)) -> x(2)).toMap
   228	    assert(core(("x", r(9))) == 0 && core(("x", r(8))) == 1 && core(("y", r(8))) == 0)
   229	  }

   231	  test(
   232	    "k-core: known coreness (clique, cycle, star, tree, Petersen, clique with a tail) and seeded graphs"
   233	  ) {
   234	    val petersen =
   235	      Vector((0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 3), (3, 4), (4, 0), (5, 7), (7, 9), (9, 6), (6, 8), (8, 5)) ++
   236	        (0 to 4).map(i => (i, i + 5))
   237	    val known: G = (
   238	      Vector.empty,
   239	      clique("k5", 1 to 5) ++
   240	        (1 to 6).map(i => ("c6", r(i), r(i % 6 + 1))) ++
   241	        (2 to 7).map(i => ("star", r(1), r(i))) ++
   242	        Vector(
   243	          ("tree", r(1), r(2)),
   244	          ("tree", r(1), r(3)),
   245	          ("tree", r(3), r(4)),
   246	          ("tree", r(3), r(5))
   247	        ) ++
   248	        petersen.map((a, b) => ("petersen", r(a), r(b))) ++
   249	        clique("tail", 1 to 4) ++ Vector(
   250	          ("tail", r(4), r(5)),
   251	          ("tail", r(5), r(6)),
   252	          ("tail", r(6), r(5))
   253	        )
   254	    )
   255	    val (out, _) = KCore(nodesDf(known._1), edgesDf(known._2), 50)
   256	    val got = rows(out, "window", "node", "degree", "coreness")
   257	      .map(x => (x(0).toString, x(1).toString) -> (x(2).asInstanceOf[Long], x(3).asInstanceOf[Int]))
   258	      .toMap
   259	    def all(w: String) = got.filter(_._1._1 == w).values.map(_._2).toSet
   260	    assert(all("k5") == Set(4) && all("c6") == Set(2) && all("star") == Set(1))
   261	    assert(all("tree") == Set(1) && all("petersen") == Set(3))
   262	    assert((1 to 4).forall(i => got(("tail", r(i)))._2 == 3))
   263	    assert(got(("tail", r(5)))._2 == 1 && got(("tail", r(6)))._2 == 1)
   264	    // degree is distinct coauthors: the duplicated 5-6 edge counts once.
   265	    assert(
   266	      got(("tail", r(5)))._1 == 2L && got(("tail", r(6)))._1 == 1L && got(("star", r(1)))._1 == 6L
   267	    )
   268	    // Seeded graphs, two windows sharing node IDs, against the reference peeling.
   269	    val (g1n, g1e) = generated("s1", 31L, 25, 60)
   270	    val (g2n, g2e) = generated("s2", 32L, 25, 35)
   271	    val g: G = (g1n ++ g2n, g1e ++ g2e)
   272	    val (sOut, _) = KCore(nodesDf(g._1), edgesDf(g._2), 50)
   273	    val seeded = rows(sOut, "window", "node", "coreness")
   274	      .map(x => (x(0).toString, x(1).toString) -> x(2).asInstanceOf[Int])
   275	      .toMap
   276	    val want =
   277	      adjacency(g).toVector.flatMap((w, adj) => refCoreness(adj).map((n, c) => (w, n) -> c)).toMap
   278	    assert(seeded == want)
   279	  }

   281	  test(
   282	    "communities: label propagation on known graphs equals the hand-worked and reference labels"
   283	  ) {
   284	    // Two 4-cliques joined by one edge (4-5), two disjoint triangles, one pair, one isolated node.
   285	    val known: G = (
   286	      Vector(("w", r(20))),
   287	      clique("w", 1 to 4) ++ clique("w", 5 to 8) ++ Vector(("w", r(4), r(5))) ++
   288	        clique("w", 10 to 12) ++ clique("w", 13 to 15) ++ Vector(("w", r(17), r(16)))
   289	    )
   290	    val (labels, rounds) = LabelPropagation(nodesDf(known._1), edgesDf(known._2), 10)
   291	    val got = rows(labels, "node", "label").map(x => x(0).toString -> x(1).toString).toMap
   292	    val expected =
   293	      (1 to 4).map(i => r(i) -> r(1)) ++ (5 to 8).map(i => r(i) -> r(5)) ++
   294	        (10 to 12).map(i => r(i) -> r(10)) ++ (13 to 15).map(i => r(i) -> r(13)) ++
   295	        Seq(r(16) -> r(16), r(17) -> r(16), r(20) -> r(20))
   296	    assert(got == expected.toMap)
   297	    assert(rounds.last.values.sum == 0L, "did not converge")
   298	    // Seeded graphs against the synchronous reference, including the round count.
   299	    Vector(generated("p", 41L, 20, 30), generated("q", 42L, 16, 40)).foreach { g =>
   300	      val (l, rs) = LabelPropagation(nodesDf(g._1), edgesDf(g._2), 10)
   301	      val (want, wantRounds) = refLabels(adjacency(g).values.head, 10)
   302	      assert(rows(l, "node", "label").map(x => x(0).toString -> x(1).toString).toMap == want)
   303	      assert(rs.size == wantRounds, s"${rs.size} rounds, reference $wantRounds")
   304	    }
   305	    // Row order and partitioning do not change the labels.
   306	    val shuffled = edgesDf(scala.util.Random(7L).shuffle(known._2)).repartition(3)
   307	    val (again, _) = LabelPropagation(nodesDf(known._1).repartition(2), shuffled, 10)
   308	    assert(rows(again, "node", "label").map(x => x(0).toString -> x(1).toString).toMap == got)
   309	    // The cap: one round only is recorded as not converged, with the round-1 labels kept.
   310	    val (capped, cr) = LabelPropagation(nodesDf(known._1), edgesDf(known._2), 1)
   311	    assert(cr.size == 1 && cr.head.values.sum > 0)
   312	    assert(
   313	      rows(capped, "node", "label").map(x => x(0).toString -> x(1).toString).toMap.apply(r(5)) == r(
   314	        4
   315	      )
   316	    )
   317	  }

   319	  test(
   320	    "bridge measures: hand-computed degree, cross-community degree, communities, participation"
   321	  ) {
   322	    val es =
   323	      clique("w", 1 to 4) ++ clique("w", 5 to 8) ++ Vector(("w", r(4), r(5)), ("w", r(5), r(4)))
   324	    val labels = df(
   325	      "window STRING, node STRING, label STRING",
   326	      ((1 to 4).map(i => Seq("w", r(i), r(1))) ++ (5 to 8).map(i => Seq("w", r(i), r(5))) :+
   327	        Seq("w", r(9), r(9)))*
   328	    )
   329	    val b = rows(
   330	      Bridges(labels, edgesDf(es)),
   331	      "node",
   332	      "degree",
   333	      "crossCommunityDegree",
   334	      "communitiesConnected",
   335	      "participationCoefficient"
   336	    ).map(x => x.head.toString -> x.tail).toMap
   337	    // r04: coauthors 1, 2, 3 (own community) and 5 (other): 1 - ((3/4)^2 + (1/4)^2) = 0.375.
   338	    assert(b(r(4)) == Seq(4L, 1L, 2L, 0.375))
   339	    assert(b(r(5)) == Seq(4L, 1L, 2L, 0.375))
   340	    assert(b(r(1)) == Seq(3L, 0L, 1L, 0.0))
   341	    assert(b(r(9)) == Seq(0L, 0L, 0L, 0.0))
   342	    // Seeded graph: every value within its definition's bounds.
   343	    val g = generated("s", 51L, 20, 35)
   344	    val (l, _) = LabelPropagation(nodesDf(g._1), edgesDf(g._2), 10)
   345	    val adj = adjacency(g)("s")
   346	    val lab = rows(l, "node", "label").map(x => x(0).toString -> x(1).toString).toMap
   347	    rows(
   348	      Bridges(l, edgesDf(g._2)),
   349	      "node",
   350	      "degree",
   351	      "crossCommunityDegree",
   352	      "communitiesConnected",
   353	      "participationCoefficient"
   354	    ).foreach { x =>
   355	      val n = x(0).toString
   356	      val k = adj(n).size.toLong
   357	      val cross = adj(n).count(u => lab(u) != lab(n)).toLong
   358	      val comms = adj(n).map(lab).size.toLong
   359	      val pc =
   360	        if k == 0 then 0.0
   361	        else
   362	          1.0 - adj(n).toVector.groupBy(lab).values.map(v => math.pow(v.size.toDouble / k, 2)).sum
   363	      assert(x(1) == k && x(2) == cross && x(3) == comms, x)
   364	      assert(math.abs(x(4).asInstanceOf[Double] - pc) < 1e-12, x)
   365	      if comms > 0 then assert(x(4).asInstanceOf[Double] <= 1.0 - 1.0 / comms + 1e-12, x)
   366	    }
   367	  }

   369	  test(
   370	    "Spark window summaries of known graphs, an empty window included, pass the storage identities (repair-01 D10)"
   371	  ) {
   372	    // Cross-module: the D10 identities in storage must accept what Spark really produces.
   373	    val ws = cfg.graph.windows
   374	    val graph: G = (
   375	      Vector(("all-years", r(30)), ("all-years", r(31)), ("2000-2014", r(30))),
   376	      clique("all-years", 1 to 4) ++ clique("all-years", 5 to 8) ++ Vector(
   377	        ("all-years", r(4), r(5))
   378	      ) ++
   379	        Vector(("all-years", r(10), r(11))) ++
   380	        (1 to 5).map(i => ("2000-2014", r(i), r(i + 1)))
   381	      // 2015-2026 has no node and no edge.
   382	    )
   383	    val nodes = nodesDf(graph._1)
   384	      .union(
   385	        edgesDf(graph._2).select(col("window"), col("a").as("node"))
   386	      )
   387	      .union(edgesDf(graph._2).select(col("window"), col("b").as("node")))
   388	      .distinct()
   389	    val edges = edgesDf(graph._2)
   390	    val (comp, _) = MinLabelComponents(nodes, edges, 50)
   391	    val (lab, lr) = LabelPropagation(nodes, edges, cfg.analytics.communityMaxIterations)
   392	    val summary = CorpusWindowSummaryJob.summarize(
   393	      CoauthorGraph.windows(spark, ws),
   394	      nodes.withColumnRenamed("node", "researcherId"),
   395	      edges.withColumn("jointPublicationCount", lit(1L)),
   396	      comp
   397	        .groupBy(col("window"), col("root").as("componentId"))
   398	        .agg(count(lit(1)).as("componentSize")),
   399	      lab
   400	        .groupBy(col("window"), col("label").as("communityId"))
   401	        .agg(count(lit(1)).as("communitySize")),
   402	      3
   403	    )
   404	    val run = StableIds.runId("adv-spark-window-summary", "c", "config-v1", Seq("s"), 1)
   405	    val loadRun = StableIds.runId("adv-spark-load", "c", "config-v1", Seq("l"), 1)
   406	    val converged = lr.last.values.sum == 0L
   407	    val got = summary.collect().toVector.map { x =>
   408	      def l(c: String) = x.getAs[Long](c)
   409	      def d(c: String) = Option(x.getAs[java.lang.Double](c)).map(_.doubleValue)
   410	      val w = x.getAs[String]("window")
   411	      val t = ws.find(_.name == w).get
   412	      WindowSummary(
   413	        w,
   414	        t.startYear,
   415	        t.endYear,
   416	        l("researchers"),
   417	        l("coauthorEdges"),
   418	        l("sumJointPublicationCount"),
   419	        l("components"),
   420	        l("largestComponentSize"),
   421	        l("singletonResearchers"),
   422	        l("communities"),
   423	        l("largestCommunitySize"),
   424	        l("singletonCommunities"),
   425	        l("communitiesAtLeastMinSize"),
   426	        Option(x.getAs[String]("largestComponentId"))
   427	          .map(id => "comp-" + StableIds.hash("c", w, id)),
   428	        Option(x.getAs[String]("largestCommunityId"))
   429	          .map(id => StableIds.communityId("graph-v1", w, t.startYear, t.endYear, id)),
   430	        x.getAs[Int]("years"),
   431	        d("largestComponentFraction"),
   432	        d("largestCommunityFraction"),
   433	        3,
   434	        converged,
   435	        lr.size,
   436	        "synthetic nodes of the work order 4 known graphs",
   437	        WindowSummarySourceRuns(run, run, run, run, run, "er-v2"),
   438	        Provenance(
   439	          SchemaVersions.Provenance,
   440	          SourceType.DERIVED,
   441	          w,
   442	          StoreWorld.snapshot,
   443	          run,
   444	          Some("graph-v1"),
   445	          None
   446	        ),
   447	        WindowSummaries.SchemaVersion
   448	      )
   449	    }
   450	    assert(got.map(_.window).toSet == ws.map(_.name).toSet)
   451	    val all = got.find(_.window == "all-years").get
   452	    assert(
   453	      (
   454	        all.researchers,
   455	        all.coauthorEdges,
   456	        all.components,
   457	        all.largestComponentSize,
   458	        all.singletonResearchers
   459	      ) ==
   460	        (12L, 14L, 4L, 8L, 2L)
   461	    )
   462	    assert(
   463	      (all.communities, all.largestCommunitySize, all.communitiesAtLeastMinSize) == (5L, 4L, 2L)
   464	    )
   465	    val empty = got.find(_.window == "2015-2026").get
   466	    assert(
   467	      empty.researchers == 0L && empty.components == 0L && empty.largestComponentFraction.isEmpty
   468	    )
   469	    val key = S3Layout.windowSummary(StoreWorld.snapshot, loadRun)
   470	    val parsed = WindowSummaries.parse(key, WindowSummaries.render(got), ws)
   471	    assert(parsed.isRight, parsed.left.toOption.map(_.take(5)))
   472	  }
